  A main image in a place of rest, trap, death, tomb or extinction that is also punished or controlled by the Day or a moving line riding its strength: the patient will surely die; a main image that changes into death, tomb, Void or extinction is the same. Even with a good doctor there is no effect.
- **LY.ILL.YM.10** 卦是反吟、伏吟、入墓、入绝的，也治不好，即使能治好也会落下病根。病没多久的，遇到三冲就好；病已经久了的，遇到三冲就死；这三种情形不论用神的衰旺。除此之外，就专门看重用神：遇到动散、月破、克空、日破、受伤没有救援、脱气的，危险；遇到日、月、动爻、变爻里有一个能救的，危险了又能转安。注里给了每一种的例子（七种情形都是大凶，得到日、月、动爻、变爻里有一个能救的，就要反过来看）。
  A hexagram that is Reversed, Hidden, in a tomb or in Extinction is likewise untreatable, and even if it can be treated, it becomes a lingering ailment. If the illness is not old, the three clashes cure it; if it is already long, the three clashes bring death; in these three cases the strength or weakness of the Subject is not considered. Apart from this, the Subject is what matters above all: if it meets moving-and-scattering, Month-break, control-into-Void, Day-break, being wounded without help, or drained of qi, it is in danger; if any one of the Day, Month, moving or changed lines can rescue it, danger turns again to safety. The note gives an example of each (the seven cases are all great ill omens, but if any one of the Day, Month, moving or changed lines can rescue, then look at it the other way round).
- **LY.ILL.YM.11** 从占验来看，四灭、四没的卦不死，十卦不死，没有鬼、没有财的不死，身空、命空的不死，遇到凶煞的不死；那么人之所以死，一定是用神死了他就死，用神生旺他就活。灭没：春天需、蒙，夏天观、蛊，秋天剥、节，冬天旅、临。十卦：明夷、观、贲、大畜、丰、同人、蛊、夬、需、临。凶煞：三坵五墓、丧车这一类。比如占父亲的病，六月得观卦，凭什么会死？用爻当日、月，卦里没有财、鬼，有什么凶？因为用爻坏而没有鬼，即使祈祷也得不到保佑；用爻旺而没有鬼，不祈祷也安康；没有财而用爻绝，天禄已尽；没有财而用爻旺，只是吃得少。甲子旬空亥，难道亥年生的人就不能问病？寅月破申，难道申年生的人就不能占病？所以应爻（空）不必作戒；身空的，有世爻、卦身可以凭据；本命不出现的，有变卦、伏爻可以取用。大体上专心看用爻来定生死安危，才真正得到《易》的宗旨，神煞就更轻了。
  Examining what divinations have proved: with the Four Extinguishing and Submerging hexagrams he does not die; with the ten hexagrams he does not die; with no ghost and no Wealth he does not die; with the Body Void or the destiny Void he does not die; with malign stars he does not die; so what he dies of must be that he dies when the Subject dies and lives when the Subject lives. The Extinguishing and Submerging: in spring Xu and Meng, in summer Guan and Gu, in autumn Bo and Jie, in winter Lü and Lin. The ten hexagrams: Mingyi, Guan, Bi (Adornment), Daxu, Feng, Tongren, Gu, Guai, Xu and Lin. Malign stars are such as Three Mounds and Five Tombs and the Funeral Carriage. For example, in divining a father's illness, if in the sixth month one obtains Guan, from what would he die? The Subject line sits on the Day and Month and the hexagram has neither Wealth nor ghost: what ill omen is there? For if the Subject line is spoiled and there is no ghost, prayer brings no protection; if strong and no ghost, he is well without praying; with no Wealth and the Subject in Extinction the heavenly emolument has ended; with no Wealth and the Subject strong he merely takes little food. If the Jiazi decade leaves Hai Void, may a person of the Hai natal branch not ask about illness? If the Yin month is a Month-break against Shen, may a person of the Shen natal branch not divine illness? So the Other line need not be a warning; if the Body is Void, the Self and Body can be relied on; if the natal branch does not appear, the changed hexagram and hidden lines can be taken. In general, to concentrate on the Subject line to fix life and death, safety and danger, truly grasps the intent of the Yi; the spirit-stars weigh lighter still.
- **LY.ILL.YM.13** 游魂、归魂的卦，在外做客时保得住，在家里却保不住；震、巽、坤、艮的卦，对老人危险，对年轻人不危险；女终、男穷（归妹、未济）不适合壮年人来问。注：游魂是去、归魂是回来，如果病在旅途中，路上就没有忌讳；震象棺，巽象椁，艮象坟冢，坤象坟墓，对上了年纪的人是大忌，有所准备反而更凶；未济、归妹，壮年人不适合；但也要兼用神参看。
  In Wandering Soul and Returning Soul hexagrams he is safe abroad but not at home; in Zhen, Xun, Kun and Gen he is in danger if old but not if young; 'the woman's end' and 'the man's exhaustion' (Guimei and Weiji) are not fitting for a divination by a man in his prime. The note: Wandering is going out and Returning is coming back, and if the illness falls on a journey the road holds no taboo; Zhen is the coffin, Xun the outer coffin, Gen the mound and Kun the grave, a great taboo for the old, and to be prepared is worse; Weiji and Guimei are not fitting for the prime of life; but one must also consult the Subject.
- **LY.ILL.YM.14** 忌神发动了，而原神也动，叫做二生；原神动了，而仇神也动，叫做两病；忌神、仇神动了，原神暗中动，是生路；没有忌神动，而用神明明白白地坏了，是死路。注举例：未月丙申日，占母亲的病，得同人变噬嗑，申是忌神本来克卯，亥是原神，发动泄掉忌神的气而生卯木用爻，这是二生；卯月乙卯日，占妻子的病，得同人变咸，子孙发动生财，本来是原神帮我，但卯木仇神来克戌爻，又多一层病；未月壬寅日，占丈夫的病，得同人变既济，兄、子都动，伤官本应病重，但寅申冲财，暗发生亥，后来病好起来；卯月丙寅日，占儿子本人，得坤变损，卦里有父、兄发动，木来救子孙，但酉金明明受日绝、月破，所以救不了。
  The Adversary moves and the Support also moves: this is called the double life; the Support moves and the enemy spirit also moves: the double illness; the Adversary and enemy spirit move and the Support stirs in secret: a road to life; the Adversary does not move but the Subject is plainly spoiled: a road to death. The note's examples: in the Wei month on the Bingshen day, a mother's illness was divined and Tongren changing into Shihe obtained: Shen is the Adversary and by nature controls Mao, while Hai is the Support and moves to drain the Adversary's qi and generate the Mao Wood Subject line — the double life; in the Mao month on the Yimao day, a wife's illness was divined and Tongren changing into Xian obtained: Output moves to generate Wealth, which should have been the Support helping me, but Mao Wood, the enemy spirit, controls the Xu line, adding one more illness; in the Wei month on the Renyin day, a husband's illness was divined and Tongren changing into Jiji obtained: Peer and Output both move and harm Pressure, so it should be grave, but Yin clashes Shen Wealth and secretly stirs it to generate Hai, and later he recovered; in the Mao month on the Bingyin day, a son himself was divined and Kun changing into Sun obtained: Resource and Peer move, and Wood comes to rescue Output, but You Metal is plainly in Extinction by the Day and in Month-break, so it could not be saved.
- **LY.ILL.YM.15** 飞爻上的用神绝了，但伏爻上的用神生旺，不危险；内卦的用神没了，但外卦的用神还在，不危险；卦爻里没有用神，但日、月里有用神，不危险。注举例：寅月丙寅日，占兄弟，得乾变需，申飞在用爻上，日破月破，却不知道酉伏在戌下，火生土而土生金，所以说不危险；辰月辛卯日，占儿子本人，得未济，外卦的未虽旬空，内卦的辰当月建，后来病好了；午月戊寅日，占妻子本人，得既济，卦里虽然没有用爻，但月是午、日是寅，原神和用神都出现，当月病就好了。
  If the Subject on the flying line is in Extinction but the Subject on the hidden line is born, there is no danger; if the Subject in the inner trigram is lost but the Subject in the outer trigram remains, there is no danger; if there is no Subject among the lines of the hexagram but the Day or Month holds the Subject, there is no danger. The note's examples: in the Yin month on the Bingyin day, a brother was divined and Qian changing into Xu obtained: Shen stood on the Subject line as the flying line, in Day-break and Month-break, but it was not noticed that You lay hidden beneath Xu, and Fire generates Earth and Earth generates Metal, so it is said there is no danger; in the Chen month on the Xinmao day, a son himself was divined and Weiji obtained: Wei in the outer trigram was in Void, but Chen in the inner trigram stood on the Month, and later he recovered; in the Wu month on the Wuyin day, a wife herself was divined and Jiji obtained: though there was no Subject line in the hexagram, the month was Wu and the day Yin, and the Support and the Subject both appeared, and that month she recovered.
- **LY.ILL.YM.16** 日、月、动爻克用爻，这叫做不得救，但用爻变出生它、扶它的，不危险；日、月、动爻对用神不生也不克，而用神变出克它的，就危险了。所以说：用神有一个生它的就活，有一个死的就死。注：这是说用神变生、变克是最要紧的办法，但变出的爻也应当没有毛病，才是真有救。比如未月甲辰日，占儿子本人，得咸变蹇，日、月克用爻，大凶，但用爻变出兄弟长生，所以不死；戌月戊申日，占父亲本人，得艮变蛊，用爻独自发动本该活，因为变出去在亥绝，就救不了了。
  If the Day, Month and moving lines control the Subject line, this is called no rescue, but if the Subject line changes into what generates or supports it, there is no danger; if the Day, Month and moving lines neither generate nor control the Subject, but the Subject changes into what controls it, it is in danger. So it is said: if the Subject has one source of life it lives, and if it has one death it dies. The note: this says that the Subject changing into generation or control is the most important method, but the changed line must also be without flaw for the rescue to be real: for example, in the Wei month on the Jiachen day, a son himself was divined and Xian changing into Jian (Obstruction) obtained; the Day and Month control the Subject line, a great ill omen, but the Subject changed into Peer at Birth, so he did not die; in the Xu month on the Wushen day, a father himself was divined and Gen changing into Gu obtained: the Subject line moved alone and should have lived, but it changed into Extinction at Hai and so could not be saved.
- **LY.ILL.YM.17** 卦里没有用神，说是凶兆，但得到原神发动就不会死，因为它是在趋向生路；忌神发动而用神伏藏，反而吉，因为它避开了克；虽然如此，也必须得到日、月的救助。注举例：酉月戊申日，占妻子本人，得睽变损，卦里没有用爻，本来不是吉兆，喜的是子孙发动，到冬天病好了，这叫趋生；亥月甲申日，也是占妻子本人，得姤变升，忌神发动，本来是凶兆，喜的是用神不出现，伏在月建下面，后来到子月壬辰日，病势逐渐缓解，因为申子辰会成水局，忌神合成了原神，这叫避克。
  A hexagram without the Subject is said to be an ill omen, but if the Support moves he does not die, because he is heading toward life; if the Adversary moves and the Subject is hidden it is instead auspicious, because it avoids the control; even so, it must also gain the rescue of the Day and Month. The note's examples: in the You month on the Wushen day, a wife herself was divined and Kui changing into Sun obtained: the hexagram had no Subject line and so was not auspicious, but happily Output moved, and by winter she recovered — this is 'heading toward life'; in the Hai month on the Jiashen day, again a wife herself was divined and Gou changing into Sheng obtained: the Adversary moved, which was an ill omen, but happily the Subject did not appear and was hidden beneath the Month, and later on the Renchen day of the Zi month the illness gradually eased, for Shen, Zi and Chen meet to form a Water frame and the Adversary is combined into the Support — this is 'avoiding control'.
- **LY.ILL.YY.05** 用爻遇到白虎、鬼，以及天刑、飞廉、大杀、三坵五墓、丧门、吊客、死炁、死符、丧车、浴盆这些凶煞，它们发动而临在用爻上或发动来克用爻，一定死。忌神发动的，遇到生忌神的那天死；忌神安静的，遇到忌神当值的那天死。此外用爻随鬼入墓、财爻发动帮鬼伤用、世应都落空、财旺而用爻落空、鬼旺而命爻绝、鬼动变入墓、月卦变入墓、用爻变入墓绝、用爻遇月绝，都是死。
  If the Subject line meets the Tiger, the ghost, and the malign stars Heavenly Punishment, Feilian, Great Killing, Three Mounds and Five Tombs, Mourning Gate, Guest of Condolence, Death Qi, Death Tally, Funeral Carriage and Bath Basin, and they move to sit on it or move to control it, he will surely die. If the Adversary moves, he dies on the day that generates the Adversary; if the Adversary is still, he dies on the day the Adversary is on duty. Besides, he dies if the Subject line follows the ghost into a tomb, Wealth moves to help the ghost and harm the Subject line, the Self and the Other are both Void, Wealth is strong and the Subject line Void, the ghost is in Birth and the destiny in Extinction, the ghost moves and changes into a tomb, the month-hexagram changes into a tomb, the Subject line changes into a tomb or Extinction, or the Subject line meets Extinction in the Month.
- **LY.ILL.YY.06** 从卦象断死：卦身入墓在世爻的（损卦）；本宫的外卦入墓在内卦的（遁、豫、升、蛊、井、大有、咸）；世爻坐在本宫的墓上的（噬嗑、蒙、泰、归妹）；世爻坐在鬼的墓上的（乾、丰、中孚、升、家人、旅）；世爻坐在财的墓上的（财是禄命，最忌刑、冲、墓、绝；观、解、随、益、泰、兑）；卦变入墓、绝的；用爻遇月破的，都是死。
  Death judged from the hexagram: the Body's tomb at the Self (the hexagram Sun); the outer trigram of the home palace having its tomb in the inner trigram (Dun, Yu, Sheng, Gu, Jing, Dayou, Xian); the Self sitting on the tomb of the home palace (Shihe, Meng, Tai, Guimei); the Self sitting on the ghost's tomb (Qian, Feng, Zhongfu, Sheng, Jiaren, Lü); the Self sitting on the tomb of Wealth (Wealth is the salary and destiny and fears punishment, clash, tomb and extinction; Guan, Xie, Sui, Yi, Tai, Dui); the hexagram changing into a tomb or Extinction; and the Subject line meeting Month-break: in each case he dies.
- **LY.ILL.YY.08** 黄泉杀：春天是大畜、小畜、履、井、复，夏天是遁、睽、临，秋天是明夷、蛊、旅，冬天是遁、既济、明夷、归妹。四灭卦：春天蒙、夏天蛊、秋天剥、冬天旅。四没卦：春天需、夏天观、秋天节、冬天临。棺椁杀：恒和益。以上这些卦，占病的人逢到就会死。
  The Yellow Springs killer: in spring Daxu, Xiaoxu, Lu, Jing and Fu; in summer Dun, Kui and Lin; in autumn Mingyi, Gu and Lü; in winter Dun, Jiji, Mingyi and Guimei. The Four Extinguishing hexagrams: Meng in spring, Gu in summer, Bo in autumn, Lü in winter. The Four Submerging hexagrams: Xu in spring, Guan in summer, Jie in autumn, Lin in winter. The Coffin killer: Heng and Yi. One who divines about illness and meets any of these hexagrams dies.
- **LY.ILL.YY.10** 男的忌讳在官鬼长生的那天得病，女的忌讳在官鬼沐浴的那天得病。如果没有青龙、福德、月解、天医发动来生合用爻，鬼爻入墓、绝的那天一定死。晁以道说：用爻、命爻不上卦的，死。还有，男的忌讳鬼落空，女的忌讳财落空；少年占的卦忌讳死、囚、休、废，老人占的卦忌讳旺相。
  A man should fear falling ill on the day the Pressure line is born, and a woman on the day the Pressure line is at Bath. If none of the Dragon, Fortune, Month Release or Heavenly Doctor moves to generate or combine with the Subject line, he will surely die on the day the Pressure line is in its tomb or Extinction. Chao Yidao says: if the Subject line and the destiny line do not appear in the hexagram, he dies. Further, a man should fear the ghost being Void and a woman Wealth being Void; a young person's hexagram fears Death, trapped, resting and abandoned, and an old person's hexagram fears being strong.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.01** 断病的生死，全凭用神，别的都不验：只看卦名的（得明夷、大过、观、贲、蛊、夬、丰、同人、大畜、需就断必死），说本命临死气丧门、寿衣棺木要早备的，说螣蛇动主死、白虎动主丧的，都不灵。恶煞凶神就算临了身命，只要用神、世爻和身爻旺相，你说他是生是死？不看用神也能断生死的，只有卦变六冲一种。
  Life and death in an illness are judged from the Subject alone, and nothing else proves true: judging from the hexagram name alone (Mingyi, Daguo, Guan, Bi, Gu, Guai, Feng, Tongren, Daxu and Xu being called surely fatal), saying that a natal line on the death spirit or the mourning gate means shroud and coffin must be ready early, saying that a moving Coiling Snake means death and a moving White Tiger mourning: none of these is reliable. When fierce spirits and ill stars already sit on the body and the natal line, yet the Subject and the Self and body lines are strong, who is to say he lives or dies? The one case in which life and death may be judged without looking at the Subject is a hexagram changing into a Six-Clash hexagram.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.02** 久病遇到卦是六冲、又变六冲，不管用神旺衰，是治不好的病；近病遇到它，不吃药也会好。久病遇冲必死，六冲变六冲更是如此，就算用神临着月建、又有生它的爻，也活不了。
  In a long illness, a hexagram that is Six-Clash and changes into Six-Clash is an incurable illness whatever the strength of the Subject; a recent illness that meets it recovers without medicine. A long illness meeting a clash surely means death, and Six-Clash changing into Six-Clash all the more, so that even if the Subject sits on the Month and a line generates it, the patient cannot live.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.03** 六冲卦变成六冲卦，新得的病也主危亡，是因为变出了回头克：比如巽木变乾金、艮坤变震巽，都叫回头相克，虽然没有入墓入绝，也主危亡。如果变出比和、变出把克去掉的、或者变出回头生，近病吉、久病凶，这是六冲的缘故。除了这种冲克以外，一定要看用神。
  When a Six-Clash hexagram changes into Six-Clash, even a recent illness means danger of death, because of a returning control: Xun Wood changing into Qian Metal, or Gen or Kun changing into Zhen or Xun, are all called a returning control, and though there is no tomb or extinction, they still mean danger of death. If it changes into peace, into a control that removes the danger, or into a returning generation, a recent illness is lucky and a long one unlucky, because of the Six-Clash. Apart from this clash and control, one must look at the Subject.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.04** 自己占病，世爻是用神；占父母、兄弟的病，拿父母、兄弟做用神。近病：用神落旬空或变空，如果没有日月和动爻来冲克，冲空、实空的日子病就好；如果遇到冲克，病再重也不会死。用神逢月破，要看用神旺衰：旺的，到实破那天或出了这个月就好；衰又受克的，一定危险。久病：用神落旬空或月破的，就算用神旺相，也没法医治。
  In divining one's own illness the Self line is the Subject; in divining a parent or brother, the Resource or Peer line is the Subject. A recent illness whose Subject falls into Void or changes into Void heals on the day the Void is clashed or filled if no Day, Month or moving line clashes or controls it; if it meets clash or control, even a heavy illness does not lead to death. If the Subject meets Month-break, look at whether it is strong or weak: if strong, the patient recovers on the day the break is filled or once the month has passed; if weak and controlled, he is surely in danger. In a long illness, if the Subject falls into Void or Month-break, then even if it is strong nothing can be done to treat it.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.05** 主象落空，不能一概说没救、会死在半路，近病和久病要分开断（近病落空，空一出就好）。觉子补充他验过的：近病落旬空，如果又碰上三合、六合，一定拖成久病最后死掉。
  A Void main image cannot be judged in one breath as beyond rescue and doomed to die on the way: recent and long illness must be told apart (a recent illness that falls into Void heals when the Void is out). Juezi adds what he has verified: a recent illness that falls into Void and also meets a three-harmony or a six-combine surely turns into a long illness and ends in death.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.06** 用神变成官鬼、官鬼变成用神，要慎防不测；忌神变成用神、用神变成忌神，最难调治。自己占病，世爻不宜变鬼，也不宜变出回头克；占兄弟、妻子的病，鬼与兄弟、妻子不宜互变，兄变财、财变兄、父变子、子变父也不宜。鬼是父母的元神，父母爻动、变成鬼，是化生，病轻的马上好；久病的，鬼变父、父变鬼，都主危亡。
  The Subject changing into Pressure or Pressure changing into the Subject calls for great care against the unforeseen; the Adversary changing into the Subject or the Subject into the Adversary is hardest to treat. In one's own illness the Self line should not change into Pressure or into a returning control; in a brother's or a wife's illness Pressure and the Peer or Wealth line should not change into each other, and neither should Peer into Wealth, Wealth into Peer, Resource into Output or Output into Resource. Pressure is the Support of Resource, so Resource moving and changing into Pressure is a changing into generation and a light illness heals at once; in a long illness, Pressure changing into Resource or Resource into Pressure always means danger of death.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.07** 用神入墓、入绝，或者动了变入墓、变入绝，全看用神旺衰：旺的不必担心，衰的确实令人担忧。日月、动爻来冲克用神，也要看用神旺衰：旺相的，到冲走克神的那天就好；衰弱的，到生助克神的那天就危险。
  A Subject that enters a tomb or extinction, or moves and changes into tomb or extinction, is judged wholly by whether it is strong or weak: a strong one need not be feared, a weak one is truly a cause for worry. When the Day, Month or a moving line clashes or controls the Subject, one also sees whether the Subject is strong or weak: if strong, it heals on the day the controlling spirit is clashed away; if weak, it is in danger on the day the controlling spirit is generated or helped.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.08** 用神临在散上、遇到破、完全没有生扶、或者被脱气，这四种情形要是没有根基，少吉多凶。忌神动来克用神，如果有元神发动，就许它有救，只怕仇神又动，就主凶险危急。
  If the Subject sits on dispersal, meets a break, has no generation or support at all, or is drained of qi, and it has no root in any of these four cases, there is little luck and much ill. When the Adversary moves and controls the Subject, if the Support moves, promise rescue; the only fear is that the Enemy spirit moves as well, and then it means danger and misfortune.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.13** 用爻在绝地遇到生，危险却有救；主象衰弱得到帮助，病重又有什么妨碍。用爻受到克制、刑冲，只要日月、动爻有一个来生扶它，就是绝处逢生，临危有救。用神不宜太弱，太弱就体虚，一时难以痊愈，但只要得到生扶拱合，就算得重病，也不至于死。觉子补充：用神也不宜太旺，易理贵在中庸，过头和不及一样。
  The Subject meeting life in extinction is in danger yet rescued; a weak main image that is helped, however heavy the illness, what does it matter? If the Subject line is controlled, punished or clashed, but the Day, Month or a moving line has one that generates and supports it, this is meeting life in a place of extinction, and in danger it is rescued. The Subject should not be too weak; if too weak the body is frail and cannot quickly heal, but if it gets generation, support, embracing or combining, then even in a heavy illness he does not die. Juezi adds: the Subject should not be too strong either; the principle of the Changes prizes the mean, and going too far is like falling short.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.35** 占医生的卦里看不到医生，有两种情形。一种是治不好的病，神不让医生出现：用神变鬼、鬼变用神，忌神变六亲、六亲变忌神，用神变绝、变克、变墓，久病遇冲遇空，随着鬼入墓，这些都是没有药可医的病。另一种是马上就好的病，神也不让医生出现：近病如果不是卦得六冲，就是用神值旬空、或用神变回头生；自己占病，子孙持世，这些都是不吃药就好的。
  In a hexagram that divines a doctor, the doctor may fail to appear, in two cases. One is an illness that cannot be cured, where the spirit does not let the doctor appear: the Subject changing into Pressure or Pressure into the Subject, the Adversary into a relation or a relation into the Adversary, the Subject changing into extinction, into a control or into a tomb, a long illness meeting a clash or Void, following Pressure into the tomb; all these are illnesses that no medicine can treat. The other is an illness that heals at once, where the spirit also does not let the doctor appear: a recent illness is either in a Six-Clash hexagram, or has its Subject in Void or changing into a returning generation; in divining one's own illness, Output holds the Self line; in all these the patient recovers without medicine.

## 动变(Moving lines and what they turn into)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/疾病/动变.md`

- **LY.ILL.EL.09** 用象虽然落在弱地，如果遇上日、月来生扶，一定没有灾咎；用象如果安静，不必细究，如果发动，就必须推它的变化，变出生扶就吉，变成墓、绝就凶。歌诀说：用爻有气、元神发动，忌神纵然发动也不必惊慌；主象休囚，又加上被克被破，身体即使暂时没病也会倾覆；用神旺相又遇扶助，病即使到了危险的地步，反而主生。注说：得到用爻旺相、元神又动，日、月即使来伤它，也永远不受克，忌神虽然发动，也不成灾殃；主象如果休囚、元神又静，忌神又正在发动、或者用爻被月建、日辰所伐，身体虽然只是小病，后来一定伤及身躯；用象如果落在旺地又遇生扶，一定有起死回生的征兆。
  Though the Subject image sits in a weak place, if the Day and Month generate and support it, there is surely no fault; if the Subject is still, there is no need to examine it closely, and if it moves, one must work out its change, a change into generation or support being auspicious and a change into a tomb or Extinction ominous. The verse says: with the Subject line in qi and the Support moving, even if the Adversary moves there is no need for alarm; with the chief image resting or trapped and also controlled and broken, though the body is for the moment unharmed it will still collapse; with the Subject strong and meeting support, though the illness reaches danger it on the contrary means life. The commentary says: if the Subject line is strong and the Support moves too, then even if the Day and Month come to wound it, it is never controlled, and though the Adversary moves, it does no harm; if the chief image is resting or trapped, the Support is still, and the Adversary is moreover moving or the Subject is attacked by the Month or Day, then though the body has only a minor illness, in the end the body will be harmed; if the Subject image is in a strong place and meets generation and support, there is surely a sign of rising from death to life.
- **LY.ILL.EL.12** 忌神变生、用神变克，一丝一毫的病也恐怕要伤刑；用神旺、忌神绝，沉重的灾也立刻转轻。像这样断定，可以留传万古，何须再到海外去求明白。注说：疾病卦里，忌神旺又发动、又变出生扶，主象衰弱摇动、变成死绝，元神又被克破，怎么还有救？用神即使居于衰位，如果化出帝旺、长生，又遇上扶助，忌神虽然发动而变成克伤死绝之乡，不能制用神，病虽然沉重，旦夕可以安好，这样推没有差错。
  If the Adversary changes into generation and the Subject changes into control, even the slightest ailment may bring harm; if the Subject is strong and the Adversary in Extinction, a heavy calamity lightens at once. What is decided thus can be handed down for ten thousand ages; why then seek clarity overseas? The commentary says: in an illness hexagram, if the Adversary is strong and moving and also changes into generation and support, the chief image is weak and swaying and changes into death or Extinction, and the Support is controlled and broken, how could there be rescue? Though the Subject sits in a weak place, if it changes into Peak or Birth and meets support, then although the Adversary moves and changes into a place of control, wounding, death or Extinction and cannot restrain the Subject, the illness, however heavy, may be at ease by morning or night, and there is no error in reckoning so.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.09** 官鬼爻化出官鬼爻，是新旧两种病；鬼爻化出鬼爻，病情迁变，花样百出。旧注说：本宫官鬼还伏在别宫官鬼下面，是旧病，或者旧病没有谨慎、变出别的病，或者新旧两种病；鬼化鬼是病有进有退，或者变了症候，或者药石杂乱，或者忽冷忽热。王洪绪补充：官鬼化进神病加重，化退神病减轻。
  Pressure changing into Pressure means two illnesses, new and old; Pressure changing into Pressure shifts and changes in a hundred ways. The old commentary says: if the home palace's Pressure is still hidden beneath another palace's Pressure, it is an old illness, or through carelessness with the old illness another disease has grown from it, or it is two kinds of illness, new and old; Pressure changing into Pressure means the illness advances and retreats, or the syndrome changes, or the drugs and needles are muddled, or chills and fever are uncertain. Wang Hongxu adds: Pressure changing into an Advancing spirit, the illness grows; into a Retreating spirit, it lessens.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.23** 鬼爻落在死、绝的爻位上，病本来轻。遇到日辰或动爻来生扶，叫绝处逢生，病一定是先轻后重、快好了又犯。如果是财爻生鬼，一定是因为饮食使病加重，或者因为又和女人同房。
  A Pressure line on a death or extinction position means a light illness. When the Day or a moving line generates or supports it, this is called meeting life in a place of extinction, and the illness is surely light and then heavy again, settling and then flaring. If Wealth generates Pressure, the illness is surely made heavier by food and drink, or by being with a woman again.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.43** 鬼爻发动，病势一定重。如果鬼变入长生，就是一天比一天重的象，一直要过了帝旺的日子，才会减轻；鬼在长生的爻上发动，也是这样。如果变入衰、病，就是一天比一天轻，和子孙（福）变入长生一样，都吉。
  When a Pressure line moves, the illness is surely heavy. If Pressure changes into Birth, it is the image of growing heavier each day, and it is only after the day of its Peak has passed that it lightens; a Pressure line moving while on a Birth line is the same. If it changes into Decline or Sickness, it grows lighter each day, and this, like Output changing into Birth, is all lucky.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.45** 子孙发动制伏官鬼，病一定减轻；如果子孙变出忌爻反过来伤克，或者子孙变成官爻，一定是病稍微好了一点、不谨慎，结果又加重。
  When Output moves and restrains Pressure the illness surely lessens; but if Output changes into an Adversary line that turns back to harm, or Output changes into a Pressure line, it is surely because the illness has slightly eased and the patient is not careful, so that it grows heavy again.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.51** 卦里的动爻变出父母（文书）来冲克世爻，要自己放宽心、少劳碌，不然病马上复发，反而加重。变出财爻来冲克，要提防饮食引起复发；再加玄武，要提防女色引起复发；变出子孙来冲克，是被药耽误了；变出兄弟来冲克，一生气就复发。以上这些，如果日辰带着刑、冲，就不能照这个断，只能说是因为这个而又加重，因为日辰上就是鬼。
  When a moving line in the hexagram changes out the Resource line (documents) that clashes or controls the Self line, he should keep at ease and toil little, or the illness returns at once and grows heavier. If it changes out Wealth that clashes or controls, beware of a relapse through food; with the Dark Tortoise added, beware of a relapse through lust; if it changes out Output that clashes or controls, he has been misled by medicine; if it changes out Peer that clashes or controls, anger brings the relapse. If in all this the Day carries punishment or clash, one may not judge this way: one can say only that it grows heavier because of this, for the Day is Pressure there.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.11** 鬼变入长生、忌神变进神，要担心添灾。鬼爻发动，病势一定重，如果在日辰上长生、或动而变入长生，病势一定加重；克用神的是忌神，它动而变进神，也是这个意思；忌神变退，病就渐渐减轻。觉子补充：鬼是父母的元神，占父母的病，这病马上就好；鬼是妻子占丈夫时的用神，占丈夫的病，也主马上就好。
  Pressure changing into Birth, or the Adversary changing into an Advancing spirit: beware of added trouble. When the Pressure line moves the illness is surely heavy; if it is at Birth on the Day, or moves and changes into Birth, the illness surely grows; what controls the Subject is the Adversary, and if it moves and changes into an Advancing spirit the meaning is the same; if the Adversary changes into Retreat, the illness gradually eases. Juezi adds: Pressure is the Support of the Resource line, so in divining a parent the illness heals at once; Pressure is the Subject when a wife divines her husband, and in divining a husband the illness also heals at once.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.12** 子孙发动却变出克、或者卦是反吟，病一定反复。自己占病和占兄弟的病，如果子孙发动、制服官鬼，这病马上就好；如果子孙动而变出克，即使病好了，好了以后还会再发，是反复的象；卦是反吟的，也照这个断。
  Output moving but changing into a control, or the hexagram being a Reversed Chant: the illness surely relapses. In divining one's own illness or a brother's, if Output moves and subdues Pressure, the illness heals at once; if Output moves but changes into a control, then even if he recovers, the illness breaks out again afterward, an image of relapse; a hexagram that is a Reversed Chant is judged the same way.

## 应期(Timing: when the illness lifts or worsens, and when death may come)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/疾病/应期.md`

- **LY.ILL.EL.19** 凡是占病人哪一天痊愈，用爻临在生旺上，身体应当安好；元神值日，灾应减轻；忌象受伤，病必痊愈；助鬼伤身，遇到福解（福神来解）；随官入墓，见冲就欢喜；如果主象落在绝地，就等它逢生的时候，免去祸愆。注说：占病哪一天痊愈，要推主象。卦里元神旺相、忌客休囚而有用爻的，就取用爻值日的那天安好；如果用爻衰弱，才取生旺的时期。倘若用爻重重太旺，反而喜欢它入墓的时候；如果不太旺，又取元神值日；忌神如果动，必须遇上冲克忌神的日子，才能安康。若遇助鬼伤身，又利于子孙值日。随官入墓，还要求冲开墓的日辰。用象如果临在绝地而逢生，一定主平安。
  To divine on what day a patient recovers: with the Subject line on generation or strength, the body should be well; on the day of the Support the calamity should lessen; when the adversary image is wounded the illness surely heals; helping the ghost to harm the Self is resolved by meeting the fortune spirit; following the Pressure line into a tomb is glad to meet a clash; if the chief image sits in Extinction, wait for the time it is born, and misfortune is avoided. The commentary says: to divine on what day the illness is cured, work out the chief image. If in the hexagram the Support is strong and the adversary guest resting or trapped and there is a Subject line, take the day on which the Subject line is on duty for ease; if the Subject line is weak, take a period of generation or strength. If the Subject line is far too strong, on the contrary it is glad of the time it enters a tomb; if it is not too strong, take the day of the Support; if the Adversary moves, one must meet the day that clashes and controls the Adversary before health returns. Meeting helping the ghost to harm the Self, the day of Output on duty is again favourable. Following the Pressure line into a tomb, seek further a day that clashes the tomb open. If the Subject image sits in Extinction and meets generation, it surely means peace.
- **LY.ILL.EL.20** 病人来占，要细看凶日：用爻无气，又遭伤克，忌爻哪天逢到生助，便主身危，立在孝堂；忌神旺动、仇神发动，用神失位反而无妨；变爻怕日辰临在用爻的位置，如果挨过去就免于凄惶；元神被克灾更重，忌客逢生一定受殃；月破用爻，天命就止，纵然旺相也必死。注说：论病哪一天出现凶险，不过看用爻的生克：用爻如果无气、被日辰克的就凶，忌神动、用爻很衰的时候，遇上再生忌神的日子就死；又说卦里元神不发，忌神兴旺、仇神都动，唯独没有用爻，眼前无事，等到日后用神值日就难以回避，一定入黄泉；倘若忌神、仇神兴旺而元神同时发动，也没有主象，等到用爻值日反而无妨；再说卦里用象既衰，全靠元神相救，担心忌客来伤，日辰克制元神，灾就沉重；忌象如果逢生旺的时候，一定成凶咎；主象如果逢月破，不论衰旺，命必归阴。
  For a patient who comes to divine, examine the ominous days closely: with the Subject line lacking qi and moreover wounded, on the day the adversary line meets generation and support the body is in danger, standing in the house of mourning; the Adversary strong and moving and the enemy spirit rising, the Subject having lost its place is on the contrary all right; a change fears the Day sitting on the Subject's place, and if it is got past there is freedom from dismay; the Support controlled makes the calamity heavier, and the adversary guest meeting generation surely brings disaster; the Subject line in Month-break ends one's allotted span, and even if strong it will die. The commentary says: to judge on what day the illness turns ominous is nothing but looking at the generation and control of the Subject line: if the Subject line has no qi and is controlled by the Day it is ominous, and when the Adversary moves and the Subject line is very weak, meeting a day that again generates the Adversary means death; again, if in the hexagram the Support does not move, the Adversary thrives and the enemy spirit all move, and there is only no Subject line, nothing happens for now, but when the Subject's day comes it is hard to avoid and he surely enters the Yellow Springs; if the Adversary and enemy spirit thrive and the Support moves with them, and there is also no chief image, then waiting until the Subject line's day is on the contrary harmless; moreover, if the Subject image in the hexagram is already weak, everything depends on the Support to rescue it, and one worries that the adversary guest will come to wound it, and if the Day controls the Support the calamity will be heavy; if the adversary image meets a time of generation or strength it is sure to make misfortune; if the chief image meets Month-break, whether strong or weak, life must return to the shades.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.39** 用爻不上卦，这病一定拖着难好：出现在卦里的爻，日辰、动爻才能生它、合它，不上卦，就算有生扶也没有地方落脚，这种病也是恍惚朦胧、不爽快的病。要等用爻值日露出来，或者久病等它值年、值月才好，所以说一定拖延日子和月份。
  A Subject line that is absent from the hexagram means the illness drags on and cannot settle: a line that appears can be generated and combined by the Day and moving lines, but if it is not in the hexagram, where would generation and support land? Such an illness is vague and hazy, one that never feels clear. One must wait until the Subject line rules the day and comes into the open, or in a long illness wait for the year or month that it rules, before it heals; hence it is said to drag on for days and months.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.57** 要断病能不能好，看福神的动静；要知道命数什么时候尽，看鬼杀的旺衰。福神、鬼杀指哪一爻，两家读法不同（见分歧）。
  To decide whether an illness will heal, study whether the fortune spirit moves or is still; to know when life is used up, examine whether the Pressure killer is strong or weak. Which lines the 'fortune spirit' and the 'Pressure killer' are, the two commentaries read differently (see the dispute).
- **LY.ILL.YM.18** 问什么时候危险，有三种办法。第一，官鬼当值的日、月，大概就是完了的时候（占父母的病不这样论，因为对父母来说官鬼是原神）。第二，忌神、仇神发动，用神伏藏着躲避克制，等到用神当值的日、月它一露出来，就受仇神、忌神的伤。第三，用神月破，危险在那个月；日破，危险在那一天；用神失了气，危险在失气的时候；用神失了援助，危险在失援的那一段时期。注里举了四个例子，又总结说：冲破原神的日子、生助忌神的时候，都凶。
  There are three ways to ask the time of danger. First, the day or month that the Pressure line rules may be the end (in divining a parent's illness this is not counted, because for a parent the Pressure line is the Support). Second, the Adversary and enemy spirits move while the Subject lies hidden to escape being controlled, and when the day or month that the Subject rules arrives and it comes out, it is wounded by the enemy and the Adversary. Third, with the Subject in Month-break the danger is in that month, in Day-break on that day; with the Subject losing its qi, at that time; with the Subject losing its support, in that period. The note gives four cases and sums up: the day that breaks the Support and the time that helps the Adversary are both ominous.
- **LY.ILL.YM.19** 问哪一天转安，有六种办法：用神被困了，得到生扶，病就好转；用神落空了，等到填实，病就痊愈；用神破散了，过了破散的日、月就能保住；用神入墓的，墓被冲开，病就退；鬼被制住，祸患就消；用神伏藏的，用神出现，人就起得来。这六种是病转安的时辰。注里逐种落到日子和时期上；又说子孙值日，医药见效、祷告灵验，官鬼当值的时辰，灾患生变、神志疲困；只有占丈夫、占官的病不这样论。
  There are six ways to ask the day of ease: the Subject beleaguered, gaining generation brings recovery; the Subject Void, becoming full heals the illness; the Subject broken and scattered, it can be preserved once the day or month of the breaking is past; the tomb opened, the illness withdraws; the ghost controlled, the trouble dissolves; the Subject appearing, the body gets up. These six are the times when the illness settles into ease. The note applies each to a day or a period, and adds that when Output is on duty medicine takes effect and prayer is answered, and when the Pressure line rules the hour the trouble changes and the spirit is worn down, except that in divining a husband or an official this is not counted.
- **LY.ILL.YY.04** 用爻遇到青龙、福德、月解、天医、天喜这些吉神，临在它上面或者生它、合它，病就会好。鬼伤到用爻的，子孙生旺、当值的那天好，子孙来生用爻、合用爻的那天好，鬼被刑、害、克、冲的那天好。用爻入胎、入墓的，刑冲那个胎、墓的日子好；用爻死、绝的，生它的那天好；用爻在『病』位的，它生旺的那天好；用爻伏藏而原神旺、忌神衰的，用爻当值的那天好；用爻的墓遇空、绝遇生、克用的鬼遇克，都可以救，等鬼衰、身旺、世被生的那天好。
  If the Subject line meets the auspicious spirits Dragon, Fortune, Month Release, Heavenly Doctor or Heavenly Joy, sitting on it or generating or combining with it, he recovers. If the ghost harms the Subject line, he recovers on the day Output is strong and on duty, on the day Output generates or combines with the Subject line, and on the day the ghost is punished, harmed, controlled or clashed. If the Subject line enters Womb or a tomb, he recovers on the day that punishes or clashes the Womb or tomb; if it is in Death or Extinction, on the day that generates it; if it is at Sickness, on the day it is born or strong; if the Subject line is hidden but the Support is strong and the Adversary weak, on the day the Subject line comes on duty; and if its tomb meets Void, its Extinction meets generation, or the ghost that harms it meets control, he can be saved, and recovers on the day the ghost is weak, the Body strong and the Self generated.
- **LY.ILL.YY.07** 死的日子：忌神长生的那天死（土做用的，亥日死；木的，巳日死；火的，申日死；水的，巳日、午日死；金的，寅日死）；土鬼发动的，鬼爻长生的那天死；卦是六冲的，用爻败、死、墓、绝的那天死；用爻落空又没有气的，原神绝、忌神生的那天死；土旺盛发动克到用爻的，克用爻的那天死。用爻遇空又被冲，刚起的病马上会好，久病却难好。卦里有三种没有救的：没有火、没有财、没有子孙。
  The days of death: he dies on the day the Adversary is born (for Earth as the Subject, a Hai day; Wood, a Si day; Fire, a Shen day; Water, a Si or Wu day; Metal, a Yin day); if an Earth ghost moves, on the day the Pressure line is born; if the hexagram is Six-Clash, on a day when the Subject line is at ruin, death, tomb or Extinction; if the Subject line is Void and without force, on the day the Support is extinct and the Adversary is born; if strong moving Earth controls the Subject line, on the day that controls it. If the Subject line meets Void and is clashed, a recent illness is cured at once while a long illness is hard to cure. A hexagram has three kinds of no rescue: no Fire, no Wealth, no Output.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.16** 近病：用神落空、或变成空，到出空的日子就好；可是如果两个卦都值三合，就会拖成久病，最后死掉，死期应在把它的墓填实的年月。
  In a recent illness, if the Subject falls into Void or changes into Void, it heals on the day the Void is out; but if both hexagrams meet a three-harmony, it turns into a long illness and ends in death, the time of death falling in the year or month that fills its tomb.

## 医药(Doctors and medicine: which doctor, which remedy, when to treat)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/疾病/医药.md`

- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.12** 子孙发动，应当不用吃药自己就好；父母爻交重（发动），即使请医生也难以治好。具体说，子孙发动、没有遇到日、月、动爻、变爻的伤克，如果占用药，应当许他立刻见效，所以用「不吃药也能好」来比喻。
  If Output moves, one recovers of oneself without medicine; if Resource moves, then even calling a doctor it is hard to cure. Specifically, if Output moves and meets no harm or control from the Day, Month, moving or changed lines, then if one divines about taking medicine one may promise that it will take effect at once, so it is put as 'it can be cured without medicine'.
- **LY.ILL.EL.26** 以子孙为主，应象为凭。子孙是药剂，应爻是医生；福德发动，病必被驱走；鬼旺、福衰，灾病治不好；官鬼衰、子孙旺，病患能除；应爻空，只怕遇不到医生；子孙陷落，还愁药不对症。注说：凡是求医，子孙是用象，子孙如果发动，药就奏神功；其中官鬼太旺，子孙休囚，这个灾不会好；官鬼如果衰弱、福神兴隆，再得到应克世爻、或外卦伤内卦，一定遇到良医，没有灾不好的；如果内克外、以及世克应、子孙旺相，还行；子孙再休囚，药就没有效验，月将伤官铲有余；鬼如果遇上生财、或动，子孙纵然发动也成空虚。父爻如果发动，药力全空；父爻与兄弟同时发动，子孙靠兄弟生，那药有效；如果得到月建、或日辰克制官爻，就像遇到岐伯；倘或鬼临日月、或鬼持世卦，虽然有子孙药也无功；又如子孙、财爻并发，官鬼伏着、财爻扶它，怎么能治病？财爻、子孙纵然发动，卦里没有鬼或鬼空，仍然有效；凡是论医药，若得子孙上卦，官鬼父母两方都安，应爻不空亡，才有效。
  Output is the medicine and the Other is the doctor. The fortune spirit moving, the illness is surely driven out; the ghost strong and the fortune spirit weak, the calamity cannot be treated; the Pressure line weak and Output strong, the affliction can be removed; the Other Void, one fears only that the man is hard to meet; Output fallen, one worries still that the medicine is unsuitable. The commentary says: in seeking a doctor Output is the Subject image, and if Output moves the medicine works wonders; where the Pressure line is too strong and Output resting or trapped, the calamity is not cured; if the Pressure line is weak and the fortune spirit thriving, and further the Other controls the Self line or the outer trigram wounds the inner trigram, one is sure to meet a Lu Bian-like physician and no calamity is not cured; if the inner controls the outer, or the Self controls the Other, and Output is strong, it is still fine; if Output is again resting or trapped the medicine has no effect, the Month general wounding the official and planing off what is in excess; if the ghost meets generation of Wealth, or moves, even if Output moves it is hollow. If the Resource line moves, the power of the medicine is wholly empty; if the Resource and Peer lines move together, Output depends on Peer to generate it and the medicine is effective; if the Month or the Day controls the Pressure line, it is as if one met Qi Bo; if the ghost sits on the Day or Month, or the ghost holds the Self, then even with Output as medicine there is no effect; again, if Output and Wealth both move, the Pressure line hidden and supported by Wealth, how can the illness be treated? If Wealth and Output move but the hexagram has no ghost or the ghost is Void, it is still effective; in all discussion of medicine, it works only if Output is in the hexagram, the Pressure line and the Resource line are both at peace, and the Other is not Void.
- **LY.ILL.EL.27** 以子孙为主，世应为凭。应是病人，世是医生；子孙是药效，鬼是灾；鬼强、子孙弱，殃难解；福旺、官柔，病渐衰；应上坐着官鬼，是真疾病；身上带福，是妙医才。世如果克应，灾当痊愈；应如果生世，确实是宾主相合；子动、兄安、财静旺，仙丹妙剂多得财；应空，他不迎我去救；世陷落，我治不了他的灾。注说：医生自己来占，反而把应当作病人，世是自己，子孙是药效，鬼是灾殃：官旺子衰、或官动子静，病一定难医；子动官静、或子旺官柔，药没有不灵验的；应如果临官，病真的无法治；世如果值福，治病能痊愈。世克应爻、或内克外卦，能治好他的灾；应如果生世以及外卦生内爻、或卦逢六合，是有为而来、宾主相得；妻财旺相，子孙交重发动，鬼静兄弟安，药灵而利益加倍；应值空亡，他必定无心就我；世居空地，自己心里萎靡懒散，医恐怕做不成。
  Output is the main line, and the Self and the Other are the ground. The Other is the patient and the Self is the doctor; Output is the effect of the medicine and the ghost is the calamity; the ghost strong and Output weak, the misfortune is hard to resolve; the fortune spirit strong and the Pressure line soft, the illness gradually wanes; a Pressure line sitting on the Other means a real illness; the fortune spirit on the body means a marvellous physician. If the Self controls the Other, the calamity should heal; if the Other generates the Self, host and guest truly accord; Output moving, Peer at peace and Wealth still and strong, elixir and wonderful remedy earn much wealth; the Other Void, he does not welcome my rescue; the Self fallen, I cannot cure his calamity. The commentary says: when a doctor himself divines, on the contrary the Other is taken as the patient, the Self as himself, Output as the effect of the medicine and the ghost as the calamity: with Pressure strong and Output weak, or Pressure moving and Output still, the illness is surely hard to treat; with Output moving and Pressure still, or Output strong and Pressure soft, the medicine is never without effect; if the Other sits on Pressure, the illness is real and cannot be treated; if the Self holds the fortune spirit, the treatment can heal. The Self controlling the Other line, or the inner trigram controlling the outer, can cure his calamity; if the Other generates the Self, or the outer generates the inner line, or the hexagram meets six-combine, one comes with purpose and host and guest suit each other; Wealth strong, Output moving, the ghost still and Peer at peace, the medicine is effective and the profit doubled; if the Other is Void, he surely has no heart to come to me; if the Self sits in Void, my own heart is dispirited and lazy, and the doctor may not succeed.
- **LY.ILL.EL.28** 兄弟发动，倘若遭遇同辈阻挠；财空，不要指望谢金来；六冲，怎么能把事做完；官化官，担心病又变糟；应克世身、兄弟和朱雀发动，反而遭到是非和官司，不要开怀。注说：兄弟发动或克世爻，一定被同行霸占；木如果遇空，药金不要指望；六冲的卦，医治不能有始有终；鬼变出鬼爻、或卦里有两个官鬼都动，病重又变病，怎么能治？应如果克世，兄动、财空，又加上朱雀吵嚷，不光求不到谢礼，反而惹来闲是非。
  Peer moving, he may meet obstruction from his fellows; Wealth Void, do not look for the fee; a Six-Clash hexagram, how can the matter be finished; Pressure changing into Pressure, one worries the illness turns bad again; the Other controlling the Self, Peer and the Vermilion Bird moving, one meets quarrels and lawsuits instead, and should not be light-hearted. The commentary says: Peer moving or controlling the Self line, one is surely muscled out by fellow physicians; if Wood is Void, do not look for medicine money; in a Six-Clash hexagram the treatment cannot have a beginning and an end; if the ghost changes into a Pressure line, or the hexagram has two Pressure lines both moving, the illness changes again into another, and how can it be treated? If the Other controls the Self, and Peer moves, Wealth is Void and the Vermilion Bird adds its noise, one not only fails to get the fee but brings on idle quarrels.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.32** 断病先看子孙爻。子孙能克制官鬼这个凶煞，古人叫它解神，又叫福德，占病时它又是医药。卦里没有子孙，鬼就没有东西制伏，吃药没有效果，祷告求神也不灵。子孙要得令有力，不要衰弱、被困、死、绝；日辰、动爻来生它、扶它就吉，来冲它、破坏它就凶。替父母或丈夫占病，子孙不宜发动：动了会伤克夫星，又克伤父母爻的原神。
  In judging illness look first at Output. Output can control and restrain the Pressure killer; the ancients called it the relieving spirit and also Output-virtue, and in divining illness it is also the medicine. If there is no Output in the hexagram, Pressure has nothing to restrain it: medicine has no effect and prayer to the spirits is not answered. Output should be in season and have force, not resting, trapped, dead or extinct; if the Day or a moving line generates and supports it, it is lucky, and if they clash and spoil it, unlucky. In divining the illness of a parent or a husband, Output should not move: moving, it harms and controls the husband star and also harms the Support of the Resource line.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.35** 一卦的「身」是整个卦的主体：身上坐着子孙（福德），一定没有忧患，病势再凶险也有救，用药医治一定痊愈。
  The 'body' is the substance of the whole hexagram: if Output (Output-virtue) sits on it, there is surely nothing to fear; even if the illness looks dangerous it can be saved, and treated with medicine it will surely be cured.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.40** 子孙爻既是医药，又是酒肉。子孙落空亡、临在死绝上，或者不上卦，病中一定没有肥甘的东西调养，病也缠缠绵绵难好。如果日辰或者应爻带着子孙，来生、合世爻和用爻，一定有人送礼物、送吃的来补养。
  The Output line is both medicine and wine and meat. If Output is Void, sits on death or extinction, or is not in the hexagram, the patient surely lacks rich food to build him up during the illness, and the illness also lingers and is hard to cure. If the Day or the Other line carries Output and generates or combines with the Self and Subject lines, someone will surely send gifts or food to nourish him.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.42** 占病把子孙当解神，身上临着它是大吉的兆头；如果父母爻发动来克伤它，仍然不好（王洪绪补一句：父母爻自己被制住就没关系）。
  In divining illness Output is the relieving spirit, and its sitting on the body is a very lucky sign; if the Resource line moves to control and harm it, it is still not good (Wang Hongxu adds: if the Resource line is itself restrained, there is no harm).
- **LY.ILL.HJC.52** 日辰坐在子孙上，来生、扶用爻（王洪绪：或拱、合），病一定靠药力好起来。
  When the Day sits on Output and generates and supports the Subject line (Wang Hongxu: or embraces or combines with it), the patient will surely recover through the power of medicine.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.58** 病了不去求医，能保全性命的很少；药不对症，白白死掉的很多。占医药时，应爻是医生，子孙是药。请医生：应爻落空，医生一定不来；应爻发动来生合世爻，医生一定听请；动了又落空、变出来也是空，一定去了别处；克世、冲世，都是不来。卦里没有子孙，吃药没有效果，子孙落空也没有功效。
  One who falls ill and does not seek a doctor seldom keeps his life whole, and one who takes medicine that does not fit the illness dies in vain more often than not. In divining medicine the Other line is the doctor and Output the medicine. In calling a doctor: if the Other line is Void, the doctor surely will not come; if it moves and generates or combines with the Self line, he surely obeys the call; if it moves, is Void and changes into Void, he has surely gone elsewhere; controlling or clashing the Self line, he will not come. With no Output in the hexagram medicine has no effect, and if Output is Void it does no good either.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.59** 占药要官鬼爻安静、没有气才吉。鬼一发动，病正在最厉害的时候，就算有好医生和妙药，一时也很难见效；休囚的还稍可，旺的就更凶。要等鬼爻入墓、入绝的时候用药才有功效；勉强吃药，病一定加重。
  In divining medicine the Pressure line should be still and without force. If it moves, the illness is at its fiercest, and even with a good doctor and wonderful medicine it is hard to gain effect for the moment; if resting or trapped it is a little more bearable, and if strong so much the worse. One must wait until the Pressure line meets its tomb or extinction before medicine has any effect; if he forces himself to take medicine, the illness will surely grow heavier.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.60** 世爻落空，病人不会专心去求医；世爻无缘无故落空，请医生决请不成；勉强去做，药也没有效果。占服药时世爻落空，是药不对症。
  When the Self line is Void, the patient will not set his mind on seeking a doctor; if it is Void for no reason, the call will surely not succeed; if he forces it, the medicine also has no effect. In divining the taking of medicine, a Void Self line means that the medicine does not fit the illness.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.61** 占药，官爻没有气、子孙旺相，药能胜过病，才有效。如果子孙休囚，官爻反而旺，就是药轻病重，就算吃了，也一定不能奏效。
  In divining medicine, if the Pressure line has no force and Output is strong, the medicine can overcome the illness, and then it is effective. If Output is resting or trapped and the Pressure line is strong instead, the medicine is light and the illness heavy, and even if he takes it, it surely cannot take effect.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.62** 世爻是病人一家，应爻是医生一家。世应相合相生，不是亲戚就是朋友；相冲相克，亲戚也不往来。应旺世衰，病人一家贫困，医生一定富；应衰世旺，反过来断。
  The Self line is the patient's family and the Other line the doctor's. If they combine and generate each other, the two are kin or friends; if they clash and control each other, even kin do not visit. If the Other line is strong and the Self line weak, the patient's family is poor and the doctor surely rich; if the Other line is weak and the Self line strong, judge the reverse.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.63** 卦身和世爻都不宜临在官鬼、父母两爻上，遇到了，药就不中用。父母爻持世，可以许下经醮保禳的愿；鬼爻持世，可以祷告鬼神护佑，然后吃药才有效验。
  Neither the hexagram Body nor the Self line should sit on the Pressure or Resource lines; if they do, the medicine does not hit the mark. With Resource holding the Self line one may vow scriptures, offerings and protective rites; with Pressure holding the Self line one may pray to the ghosts and spirits for protection, and only then will the medicine be effective.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.64** 占医药，遇到官鬼变官鬼，病一定有变化：症候不一样，或者病势不稳定，用药也不见效。
  In a medicine hexagram, when Pressure changes into Pressure, the illness surely changes: the symptoms are not uniform, or the course of the illness is unsettled, and medicine also has no effect.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.65** 子孙是占药用的用神，只喜欢出现一位：有气、不落空，又有日辰、动爻扶持，就是最吉的兆头。如果卦里重叠太多，或者子孙又变成子孙，就是医生不精，用药杂乱而没有功效。
  Output is the Subject in divining medicine and is glad to appear as one only: with force, not Void, and supported by the Day and moving lines, it is the best sign. If there are too many of them in the hexagram, or Output changes again into Output, the doctor is not skilled and his medicines are mixed and do no good.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.66** 有动就有变。子孙动了，变出父母就回头来克它，变出财爻就去扶持官鬼，变出兄弟药不精洁，变出官鬼这个凶煞药反而助长病，子孙变子孙是用药太杂，不能见效。所以不如安静；如果变出的爻伤了世爻、克了用爻，一定会招来因为吃药而伤命的祸。
  Where there is movement there is change. If Output moves and changes out Resource, it turns back to control it; out Wealth, it supports Pressure; out Peer, the medicine is not refined or clean; out a Pressure killer, the medicine feeds the illness instead; Output changing into Output is medicine too mixed to work. So stillness is better; if the changed line harms the Self line or controls the Subject line, disaster through medicine, harm to life, is sure to follow.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.67** 应爻上临着官鬼，这位医生一定不是良医；如果他再来刑克世爻、身爻和主象，要提防他用错药伤人。应爻变出忌爻或官鬼，都不宜用这个人的药。
  When the Other line sits on Pressure, the doctor is surely not a good one; if it also punishes or controls the Self line, the body and the main image, beware of wrong medicine that harms. If the Other line changes into an Adversary line or into Pressure, this man's medicine is unsuitable in every case.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.69** 应爻是医生，看它带的是什么，就知道医生是哪一类人：持父母，一定是僧道人家，或者兼会别的技艺；持兄弟，是庸医，或者先要药钱；持财爻，是富贵人家的医生，或者是女医生；临子孙，是专门行医的人，或者是僧道；持太岁，一定是世代行医的；持日辰而被太岁刑克的，是刚学医的；持月建，是官医，再临子孙，用药有神效。医生年老年少，用生旺墓绝来推。
  The Other line is the doctor, and what it carries shows what sort of man he is: holding Resource, a monk or Daoist household, or one who also has other arts; holding Peer, a quack, or one who asks the price of the medicine first; holding Wealth, a doctor of wealth and rank, or a woman doctor; sitting on Output, a specialist physician, or a monk or Daoist; holding Tai Sui, a doctor from a family of doctors; holding the Day but punished or controlled by Tai Sui, a beginner in medicine; holding the Month, an official physician, and if it also sits on Output, his medicine works wonders. Whether the doctor is old or young is reasoned from birth, strength, tomb and extinction.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.70** 动爻、日辰能生飞爻、克飞爻，却不能生克伏神，因为伏神是隐藏潜伏的意思。占病遇到鬼伏在世爻下面，这病一定不能断根：就算子孙发动，也只能眼前见效，过后到伏鬼旺相的月份日子，这病一定再犯。
  The moving lines and the Day can generate and control a flying line but cannot generate or control a hidden line, for a hidden line lies out of view. In divining illness, if Pressure lies hidden under the Self line, the illness surely cannot be cut off at the root: even if Output moves, it can take effect only for the present, and after this, in the month or day when the hidden Pressure is strong, the illness will surely break out again.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.71** 卦身上坐着鬼，病就不能脱身。鬼衰弱没有气，是缠缠绵绵难好、拖延不愈的象。世爻旺相出现在卦里，却长久卧床，就算有好医生也没有效果。世爻持着鬼，以及主象、身爻临在官墓上的，也都是这样。
  With Pressure on the hexagram Body, the illness does not leave the body. If Pressure is weak and without force, it is an image of lingering, hard to heal, dragging on. If the Self line appears strong in the hexagram and yet he lies long in bed, even a good doctor has no effect. The same holds when the Self line holds Pressure, and when the main image or the body sits on the tomb of Pressure.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.72** 卦里父母爻动，子孙就不能专权，本来不吉；但卦里又不能没有父母爻，要静不要动。因为人的气脉都属父母。所以占医生，如果没有这一爻，这个医生一定是乡野草泽的医人：虽然会用药，脉理却不明白，只能凭猜测。
  When the Resource line moves in the hexagram, Output cannot hold sole power, which is in itself not lucky; yet the hexagram cannot be without the Resource line, and it should be still, not moving. For a person's breath and pulse all belong to Resource. So in divining a doctor, if this line is missing, he is surely a rustic country doctor: though he can use medicine, he does not understand the pulse and goes by guesswork.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.73** 官鬼虽然是凶煞，也不能没有。它出现在卦里，就有子孙克制它、日辰刑制它，用药有效。它不上卦，病就隐伏着，病根不知道，症候没法判定，随意用药，也难以见效。
  The Pressure line is a malign killer, yet it cannot be missing. If it appears in the hexagram, Output controls it and the Day punishes it, and medicine is effective. If it is not in the hexagram, the illness lies hidden, its root cause is unknown, its symptoms cannot be decided, and medicine used at random also hardly takes effect.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.75** 父母爻发动，子孙受制，药一定没有效。如果子孙有气，又有日辰、动爻来救（冲、合父母爻，或者克父母爻），救得有用，才可以活，但也必须多服才好。父母变成子孙，也是医生太慢。
  When the Resource line moves and Output is restrained, the medicine surely has no effect. If Output has force, and the Day or a moving line comes to rescue it (clashing or combining with the Resource line, or controlling it) so that the rescue works, he may live, but even so it is best to take much of it. Resource changing into Output is also the doctor being too slow.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.76** 看官鬼爻的五行来用药：火、土官鬼，病一定发热，宜用寒凉的药去攻；金、水官鬼，病多半发寒，一定用温热的药来治。火鬼在生旺的地方又遇到生扶，一定用大寒的药攻它，休囚死绝就用凉药；水鬼在生旺的地方又遇到合助，要用大热的药，休囚死绝就用温药。火鬼在阴宫阴爻，是阴虚火动的症候，可以用滋阴降火的药；水鬼在阳宫内卦，是血气虚损的症候，可以用补中益气的药。要会变通，其余照这个类推。
  Choose the medicine by the phase of the Pressure line. A Fire or Earth Pressure line: the illness is surely hot, and should be attacked with cold and cool medicine; a Metal or Water Pressure line: the illness is mostly cold, and is surely treated with warm and hot medicine. A Fire Pressure in a place of birth or strength that also meets generation and support must be attacked with very cold medicine, and with cool medicine if resting, trapped, dead or extinct; a Water Pressure in a place of birth or strength that also meets combination and help needs very hot medicine, and warm medicine if resting, trapped, dead or extinct. A Fire Pressure in a yin palace on a yin line is the syndrome of yin deficiency with stirring fire, and medicine that nourishes yin and lowers fire may be used; a Water Pressure in the inner trigram of a yang palace is the syndrome of depleted blood and qi, and medicine that supplements the centre and boosts qi may be used. One must be flexible; the rest follow this.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.77** 凡是占服药，看子孙在哪一爻，就知道该请哪个方向的医生：子孙在子爻，宜请北方的医生；在丑爻，宜请东北方的医生，依此类推。又如寅爻是子孙，五行属木，那位医生姓名带木字旁或草字头，或者是属虎的；就算不在东北，也都能治。其余照这个类推。
  In divining the taking of medicine, look at which line Output is on, and one knows from which direction a doctor can treat him: Output on the Zi line, a doctor from the north; on the Chou line, a doctor from the north-east, and so on. Again, if the Output line is Yin, which belongs to Wood, the doctor has a name with the wood radical or the grass radical, or was born in a tiger year; though not from the north-east, all of these can treat him. The rest follow this.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.78** 财爻管饮食，靠它养生，但它一动就会去生助鬼爻，反而被它所害。财若属水，一定忌鱼鲜和生冷的东西，药才见效；属木，忌动风的东西；属火，忌烤炒的热东西；属金，忌坚硬和咸的东西；属土，忌油腻和滑的东西。财爻不动，不可乱说。又要忌鬼爻所属生肖的东西，比如丑忌牛，酉忌鸡。
  The Wealth line governs food and drink, on which life is sustained, but when it moves it generates and helps the Pressure line and one is harmed by it instead. If Wealth is Water, fish, fresh seafood and raw or cold things must be avoided, and only then does the medicine work; if Wood, avoid things that stir wind; if Fire, roasted, fried and hot things; if Metal, hard and salty things; if Earth, greasy and slippery things. If Wealth does not move, one may not speak at random. Also avoid what belongs to the zodiac animal of the Pressure line: for example Chou avoids the ox and You avoids the fowl.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.79** 青龙是喜悦的神，再临在木爻上，来生合世爻和主象，病人一定要把家里的事丢开、放宽心怀，然后吃药才有效。
  The Azure Dragon is the spirit of joy; if it also sits on a Wood line and generates or combines with the Self line and the main image, the patient must throw off household affairs and relax his mind, and only then is the medicine effective.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.80** 财爻在外卦发动，主呕吐。如果它生合世爻、用爻，用药催吐就会好；如果在内卦，疏通利下最好。如果财爻发动来刑克世爻和主象，在外卦就是吃药吃不下，在内卦就是吃了药之后拉肚子。
  A Wealth line moving in the outer trigram means vomiting. If it generates or combines with the Self and Subject lines, treating with a medicine that makes him vomit heals it; if it is in the inner trigram, a purge that opens the bowels is best. If the Wealth line moves to punish or control the Self line and the main image, then in the outer trigram the medicine is not kept down, and in the inner trigram there is diarrhoea after the medicine.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.81** 子孙属火，又在离宫，宜用热药来治；如果在别的宫，又居外卦，一定用艾灸才会好；如果在内卦，或者虽然在外卦却属本宫的，都照这个断。
  If Output is Fire and also in the Li palace, hot medicine should be used to treat it; if it is in another palace and in the outer trigram, it is healed only by moxibustion; if it is in the inner trigram, or, though in the outer, belongs to the home palace, judge in each case by this.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.82** 子孙在坎卦发动，都宜发汗（旧注：不管是什么五行；王洪绪：子孙属水，或者在坎宫）；官鬼属木，要先散风邪，用药才有效。这只是举一个例子，五行八卦都照这个推。
  Output moving in the Kan trigram is always suited to inducing sweat (the old commentary: whatever its phase; Wang Hongxu: Output that is Water, or in the Kan palace); a Wood Pressure line calls for dispersing the wind pathogens first, and then the medicine is effective. This is only one instance, and the five phases and eight trigrams are all reasoned in this way.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.83** 用爻休囚墓绝，一定要用补药才有效。如果用爻得时旺相，又有生扶合助，就要用克伐的药来治，再补反而有害。
  If the Subject line is resting, trapped, in a tomb or extinct, only a tonic is effective. If the Subject line is in season and strong and also has generation, support and combination to help it, it must be treated with medicine that attacks and subdues, and to tonify further does harm instead.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.84** 子孙属金，适合用刀针。鬼爻属水，如果用刀针，金能生水，反而助长病势，病一定加重。土鬼不宜用热药，水鬼不宜用寒药，火鬼不宜用风药，金鬼不宜用丸药。
  If Output is Metal, knife and needle are of use. If the Pressure line is Water and knife and needle are used, Metal can generate Water and helps the force of the illness instead, and the illness surely grows heavier. An Earth Pressure avoids hot medicine, a Water Pressure cold medicine, a Fire Pressure wind medicine, and a Metal Pressure pills.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.86** 卦里有两爻子孙发动，用药不必连着服，因为它们分了权，要多服才见效；或者用两种汤药，轮流间隔着服就有效。如果占的是疮疽肿毒，可以说内服托里的药，外面敷贴。
  When two Output lines move in the hexagram, medicine need not be taken continuously, because they divide the power, and it takes much to be effective; or use two kinds of decoction and take them alternately, and it works. If the divination is about sores, carbuncles and swollen toxin, one can say to use medicine to support the inside and a poultice on the outside.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.87** 官鬼发动来冲克间爻，或者鬼在两个间爻上发动，胸膈一定不舒服，要用宽胸的汤药。间爻遇到兄弟发动，是气不顺（气逆），药宜调气。
  When Pressure moves to clash or control a between line, or moves on the two between lines, the chest and diaphragm are surely uneasy, and a decoction that eases the chest must be used. A between line meeting a moving Peer means the qi is not flowing smoothly (counterflow of qi), and the medicine should regulate qi.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.88** 卦里子孙爻空亡、官鬼衰弱安静，如果有父母爻发动来生合世爻、身爻和主象，就不用吃药。再变出子孙，不如丢开家里的事，到深山里静养，或者进寺庙道观闭门修养，不吃药也会自己安好。
  If the Output line in the hexagram is Void and the Pressure line weak and still, and a Resource line moves to generate or combine with the Self line, the body and the main image, no medicine need be taken. If it also changes into Output, better to abandon household affairs and recuperate quietly in the deep mountains, or to enter a temple or Daoist abbey and recuperate behind a closed door, and without medicine he will recover of himself.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.89** 子孙出现在卦里却落空，而第五爻动、变出不落空的子孙，不用选求名医服药，路上遇到的乡野医生反而能治好。如果子孙不出现、六爻也没有变出子孙，而日辰带着子孙来生合，想不到的时候自然有医生来治。
  If Output appears in the hexagram but is Void, and the fifth line moves and changes out an Output that is not Void, there is no need to seek a famous doctor and take medicine: a rustic doctor met on the road can cure him instead. If Output does not appear and none of the six lines changes it out, but the Day carries Output and generates or combines with it, a doctor will come to treat him unexpectedly.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.90** 世爻和应爻比和，卦里又没有子孙，这药没有损害也没有益处，吃不吃都一样，必须换一个医生，另外求治，才能痊愈。
  When the Self and Other lines are at peace with each other and there is no Output in the hexagram, this medicine neither harms nor helps, and to take it or not is the same; he must change the doctor and seek treatment elsewhere before he can be cured.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.91** 财爻、官鬼都发动，势头已经凶险，子孙又落空，吃药没有益处，就算有好医生也一定没有功效，不如不吃，病反而渐渐好起来。
  When Wealth and Pressure both move, the situation is already bad, and if Output is also Void, taking medicine does no good; even with a good doctor there will surely be no effect, and it is better not to take it, and he will gradually recover.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.92** 凡是占医药，要诚心默默祷告，问用哪个人的药有效还是无效，不必说出医生的姓氏。卜家依照这一章来断，自然没有替医生推荐的毛病，心诚没有不感通的，卦没有不灵验的，这不是彼此都安心吗？
  Whoever divines about medicine must pray silently and sincerely, asking whether the medicine of a certain person will or will not be effective, and need not state the doctor's surname. A diviner who judges by this chapter will have no abuse of touting doctors; sincerity never fails to reach, and the hexagram never fails to prove true; is that not peace of mind for both sides?
- **LY.ILL.YM.34** 占医药的总纲：拿子孙爻当用。子孙临日、月是上等，旺相是次等，休囚是下等，空、破、散、绝就是庸医。这一章开头先说了几句话：得志的时候愿意做良相，不得志的时候愿意做良医；君子借医来施展志向，做儿子的要懂得医理，吉凶取舍能不慎重吗？章里分六问细讲：占药、占医、治病、用药、往医、行道；头两问是病家问，后四问（治病、用药、往医、行道）都是医生自己问。
  The outline for divining medicine and doctors: take Output as the one to use. Output sitting on the Day or Month is best, thriving and strong is next, resting and trapped is lowest, and Void, broken, scattered or in Extinction is a mediocre doctor. The chapter opens with a few remarks: when successful one wishes to be a good minister, when not, a good doctor; the gentleman carries out his will through medicine, and a son must know medicine, so how can one not be careful in choosing between fortune and misfortune? The chapter then treats six questions in detail: divining the medicine, divining the doctor, treating the illness, using the medicine, going to treat, and the doctor's practice; the first two are asked by the patient's family and the last four (treating, using, going, practising) are all asked by the doctor himself.
- **LY.ILL.YM.35** 卦里没有子孙，药就不好；官鬼强，病就治不好。子孙强、官鬼弱，像烈日融冰；子孙弱、官鬼强，像用铅刀去攻玉。财爻动、鬼爻静，子孙动，都没有功效。鬼伏着，病根拔不掉；变出鬼，病会传变；鬼落空，药就是「老」药。鬼临世爻而兴旺，我的病就曲折反复；鬼临应爻而旺相，那副药就古怪蹊跷。注说：要审察子孙和官鬼的强弱，鬼当值日、月时，即使子孙得到生扶也不算完全好，要比较子孙能不能制住鬼，才是吉占；鬼落空是躲开了子孙，药打不中病，所以叫「老」；鬼在世，病难很快好，鬼在应，医生诡诈难信。
  Without Output the medicine is not good, and with a strong Pressure line the illness cannot be cured. Output strong and Pressure weak is a fierce sun melting ice; Output weak and Pressure strong is a lead knife attacking jade. With Wealth moving and the ghost still, or Output moving, there is no effect. A hidden ghost means the root is not removed; a change into a ghost means the illness passes on and changes; a Void ghost makes the medicine 'aged'. A ghost thriving on the Self makes my illness twist and turn, and a ghost strong on the Other makes that medicine dubious. The note says: one must examine the strength of Output and Pressure; when the ghost is on duty in the Day or Month, even if Output meets generation and support it is not wholly good, and one must compare whether Output can subdue the ghost before it is an auspicious divination; a Void ghost evades Output and so the medicine does not hit the illness, hence 'aged'; a ghost on the Self means the illness is hard to cure quickly, and a ghost on the Other means the doctor is deceitful and hard to trust.
- **LY.ILL.YM.36** 应爻指的是医生，不是拿来占能不能克住病的：所以应落空，遇不上这个医生；应被冲破，医生不专心；应散，医生的见识反倒糊涂；应克世，医生爱财、多诈；世应相冲，治疗断断续续，做不到底。
  The Other stands for the doctor, and is not for divining whether the illness will be overcome: so with the Other Void, one cannot meet this doctor; broken, the doctor is not devoted; scattered, his insight is confused instead; the Other controlling the Self, the doctor loves profit and is full of deceit; Self and Other clashing, the treatment breaks off and is not carried to the end.
- **LY.ILL.YM.37** 占药：水属的子孙旺，宜用汤剂；土属的子孙旺，宜用丸药；火像艾灸，金像针，木像药末，以这些作象。火鬼喜欢凉药，木鬼喜欢祛风的药；土对应脾，金对应肺，水对应肾，也以这些作象。占医生：要考察子孙属于哪一类，木属东、金属西，乾是僧人、坤是妇人，也以这些作象。注说：子孙是药，鬼是病，占该用什么药、是什么病，就用这个办法；如果占的是这味药吉不吉，就看子孙的衰旺，不要死板地认为子孙属土就该用丸药。占该请哪个方位、哪种人当医生，也是这个办法；单独占医生好不好，还是用子孙的强弱，比如问请僧人当医生，得乾卦而子孙落空，怎么能说吉？
  Divining medicine: a strong Water Output suits a decoction, a strong Earth Output suits pills; Fire is moxibustion, Metal needles, Wood powders, taken as the images. A Fire ghost likes cooling medicine and a Wood ghost likes wind medicine; Earth is the spleen, Metal the lung and Water the kidney, also taken as images. Divining the doctor: examine what kind the Output belongs to; Wood is east and Metal west, Qian a monk and Kun a woman, again taken as images. The note says: Output is the medicine and the ghost is the illness, and to divine which medicine to use or what the illness is, use this method; but if what is divined is whether a medicine is auspicious, use the strength of Output, and do not rigidly hold that an Earth Output must mean pills. To divine which direction and which kind of person to use as doctor the method is the same; but to divine on its own whether the doctor is good, still use the strength of Output: for instance, if one asks about a monk-doctor and obtains Qian with Output Void, how could one call it auspicious?
- **LY.ILL.YM.38** 医生问这个病能不能治，先看应爻，因为对医生来说应爻就是病人；应爻坏了、或随墓，这个病就治不好。如果占的是病人的某个亲属，仍然以用神来断。
  When a doctor asks whether an illness can be treated, the Other line comes first, for to him the Other is the patient; if the Other is spoiled or follows into the tomb, the illness cannot be cured. If what is divined is one of the patient's relations, judgment still goes by the Subject.
- **LY.ILL.YM.39** 医生问这味药能不能奏效，也是拿子孙爻当用：子孙强、鬼弱，药就见效；子孙虚、鬼实，药就没有功效。注说：和占药一样。
  When a doctor asks whether a medicine will take effect, Output is again the line to use: with Output strong and the ghost weak it takes effect, and with Output empty and the ghost full it has no effect. The note says: it is the same as divining medicine.
- **LY.ILL.YM.40** 医生问上门去治病有没有利，就从世、应来推：应克世的，病家没有情义；卦里没有财爻的，酬谢微薄；落空的，有欺骗的心思；冲破的，没有专心；散了、冲了的，不会有个结果。
  When a doctor asks whether going to treat will be profitable, judge by the Self and the Other: the Other controlling the Self means little feeling; a hexagram with no Wealth line means a meagre gift; Void means an intent to deceive; broken, no wholehearted devotion; scattered or clashed, no end.
- **LY.ILL.YM.41** 医生问自己行医的前程：财爻、官爻强，名和利就能遂愿。所以医术不精，不足以造福万物；求医不谨慎，不足以救人性命；自己不耽误别人，也不耽误自己。
  When a doctor asks about the course of his practice, if the Wealth and Pressure lines are strong, profit and fame are achieved. So medicine that is not refined cannot benefit things, and seeking a doctor without care cannot heal life; if one does not mislead others, one does not mislead oneself.
- **LY.ILL.YY.21** 内卦的世爻、用爻是病人，外卦的应爻、子孙是医生和药。外卦克内卦，应爻和子孙克世、克用的，药有效；内卦克外卦，世爻、用爻克应、克子孙的，药没有效；外卦生合内卦，应爻和子孙生合世的，药对症但见效慢；外卦应爻克内卦世爻而卦里没有子孙的，遇不到好医生。应爻带子孙发动来克世的，药有效；世克应和子孙的，是名医但药没有效；子孙克世而外卦应爻不克内卦世爻的，药没效也没害。应爻带鬼发动、刑害克冲世爻的，是用错药伤了人；子孙落在辰午酉亥自刑之地的，用药有差错；日辰、动爻冲伤子孙的，药没有效；子孙变出父母或鬼的，药收不到功；子孙变出子孙的，药杂不精；子孙带白虎煞发动的，是庸医误人。世下伏官、遇子孙发动的，药虽好而病根除不掉；世持衰鬼的，病轻而药难治。世应比和而卦无子孙、或子孙空绝的，宜换药换医；子孙有两个都动的，换医就好；鬼和子孙都落空的，不治自愈；鬼和子孙都发动的，医药和祈祷一起用；子孙和财爻都动，鬼得到帮助，病难治。
  The Self line and Subject line in the inner trigram are the patient, and the Other line and Output in the outer trigram are the doctor and medicine. If the outer controls the inner, and the Other and Output control the Self and the Subject, the medicine is effective; if the inner controls the outer, and the Self and Subject control the Other and Output, it is not effective; if the outer generates and combines with the inner, and the Other and Output generate and combine with the Self, the medicine fits the illness but works slowly; if the Other in the outer trigram controls the Self in the inner and there is no Output in the hexagram, one does not meet a good doctor. If the Other line carries Output and moves to control the Self, the medicine is effective; if the Self controls the Other and Output, the doctor is famous but the medicine does not work; if Output controls the Self but the Other in the outer trigram does not control the Self, the medicine has no effect and does no harm. If the Other line carries the ghost and moves to punish, harm, control or clash the Self, wrong medicine harms the person; if Output stands on Chen, Wu, You or Hai, which punish themselves, the medicine is mistaken; if the Day or a moving line clashes with and wounds Output, it does not work; if Output changes into Resource or a ghost, it does not bring completion; if Output changes into Output, the medicines are mixed and not refined; if Output carries the Tiger and a killing star and moves, a mediocre doctor harms people. If Pressure is hidden beneath the Self and Output moves, the medicine is good but the root of the illness is not removed; if the Self holds a weak ghost, the illness is light but the medicine has difficulty curing it. If the Self and Other are in harmony and there is no Output, or Output is Void or in Extinction, change the medicine and doctor; if two Output lines both move, changing the doctor leads to recovery; if the ghost and Output are both Void, he recovers without treatment; if the ghost and Output both move, use doctors and prayers together; if Output and Wealth both move, the ghost is helped and the illness is hard to treat.
- **LY.ILL.YY.22** 《法法赋》说：天医生世爻的，是好医生；天医克世爻的，是庸医；日辰克医生的，医术不精；医生克日辰的，用药不恰当。世爻、用爻属金的，天医在巳有效；属木的，天医在亥有效；属火的，天医在寅有效；属水、土的，天医在申有效。
  The Fafa Fu says: if the Heavenly Doctor generates the Self, he is a good doctor; if it controls the Self, a mediocre one; if the Day controls the doctor, his medical learning is not refined; if the doctor controls the Day, the medicine used is not fitting. If the Self line and Subject line are Metal, the Heavenly Doctor at Si is effective; if Wood, at Hai; if Fire, at Yin; if Water or Earth, at Shen.
- **LY.ILL.YY.23** 《董和筮秘》说：脉要仔细察，以身爻为主。月卦临在金上，脉一定是弦、紧、代、革；临在木上，脉一定是洪、长、虚、动；临在水上，脉一定是芤、滑、濡、动；值火，脉一定是数、大、促、短；值土，脉一定是沉、实、滞、涩。
  The Dong He Shimi says: the pulse should be examined carefully, with the Body line as the master. If the month-hexagram falls on Metal, the pulse is surely wiry, tight, intermittent and leathery; on Wood, surging, long, vacuous and stirring; on Water, hollow, slippery, soft and stirring; on Fire, rapid, large, hurried and short; on Earth, deep, full, sluggish and rough.
- **LY.ILL.YY.24** 推断请来的医生是什么样的人，拿子孙做用：子孙属金，适合西方的、姓氏带金字旁的人；属木，适合东方的、姓氏带草头或木字旁的人；属水，适合北方的、姓氏带三点水的人；属火，适合南方的、姓氏带日字旁或火字旁的人；属土，适合姓氏带土字旁的，以及用丹药散剂的医生。子孙带贵人，适合有官职的人；子孙带华盖，适合僧人道士。
  To work out what kind of person the doctor is, Output is the Subject: if Output is Metal, it suits the west, a person whose surname has the metal radical; if Wood, the east, a surname with the grass or wood radical; if Water, the north, a surname with the water radical; if Fire, the south, a surname with the sun or fire radical; if Earth, a surname with the earth radical, and a doctor who works with cinnabar and powders. If Output carries the Noble, a person holding an official post; if Output carries the Canopy, a monk or Daoist.
- **LY.ILL.YY.25** 用药不要轻易乱投。官鬼属金的，适合灸，不适合丸药（火能克金，土生金）；鬼属木的，适合针，不适合汤药（金能克木，水生木）；鬼属水的，适合温药、丸药，不适合针（土克水，金生水）；鬼属火的，适合凉药、汤药，不适合草头药和饮片（水克火，木生火）；鬼属土的，适合咬咀，不适合灸（木克土，火生土）。此外，鬼伤到两个间爻的，适合宽胸膈；世爻旺又有扶助的，不妨再进补；财爻在外卦发动合用爻的，适合吐法，在内卦发动合用爻的，适合泻法；用爻、鬼爻出现或在外卦的，适合治外表，伏藏在内卦的，适合治内。
  Medicine is not to be given lightly. If the Pressure line is Metal, moxibustion suits and pills do not (Fire can control Metal, while Earth generates Metal); if the ghost is Wood, acupuncture suits and decoctions do not (Metal can control Wood, while Water generates Wood); if Water, warm medicines and pills suit and acupuncture does not (Earth controls Water, while Metal generates Water); if Fire, cool medicines and decoctions suit and herbal medicines and sliced drugs do not (Water controls Fire, while Wood generates Fire); if Earth, crushed herbs suit and moxibustion does not (Wood controls Earth, while Fire generates Earth). Besides: if the ghost harms the two between lines, ease the chest and diaphragm; if the Self is strong and supported, there is no harm in tonics again; if Wealth moves in the outer trigram and combines with the Subject line, an emetic suits, and in the inner trigram, a purgative; if the Subject line and the Pressure line appear openly or are in the outer trigram, treat the exterior, and if hidden in the inner trigram, treat the interior.
- **LY.ILL.YY.26** 周杰说：有的病是在这里受的，该治的却在那里。比如金的虎鬼冲伤用爻，是肝经受了病（金来克木），但应当治肺，不能治肝（这就是所说的去掉害我的那一个，其余照这个办法推）；木的虎鬼冲伤用爻，是脾经受病（木来伤土），该治肝，不能治脾；水的虎鬼冲伤用爻，是心经受病（水来伤火），该治肾，不能治心；火的虎鬼冲伤用爻，是肺经受病（火来伤金），该治心，不能治肺；土的虎鬼冲伤用爻，是肾经受病（土来伤水），该治脾，不能治肾（出自《松径玄谈》）。颗师说：子孙发动的，是吃错了药。
  Zhou Jie says: in some cases the illness is received in one place while the treatment lies in another. For example, if a Metal Tiger ghost clashes with and wounds the Subject line, the liver channel has received the illness (Metal comes to control Wood), yet one may treat the lungs and must not treat the liver (this is what is meant by removing what harms me; the rest follow this); a Wood Tiger ghost wounding the Subject line: the spleen channel has received the illness (Wood comes to wound Earth), treat the liver, not the spleen; a Water Tiger ghost: the heart channel (Water wounds Fire), treat the kidneys, not the heart; a Fire Tiger ghost: the lung channel (Fire wounds Metal), treat the heart, not the lungs; an Earth Tiger ghost: the kidney channel (Earth wounds Water), treat the spleen, not the kidneys (from the Songjing Xuantan). Master Ke says: when Output moves, the wrong medicine has been taken.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.10** 身上临着福德，病势虽凶险也可以医治，有两种情形。自己占病：子孙持世，不吃药也会痊愈；临空亡、月破的，到填实空、填实破的那天就好。替别人占六亲的病：不必看用神旺衰，就知道他平安，因为子孙是喜悦的神，只要旺相，如果在卦中发动，也是吉庆。觉子补充：占父母的病，子孙持世又发动，会克掉父母的元神；妻子占丈夫的病，子孙动会伤丈夫，这些情形它都不是喜悦的神，要通变来看。
  With the body sitting on Output-virtue, though the danger looks great the illness can be treated, and there are two cases. Divining one's own illness: with Output holding the Self line, one recovers without medicine; if it sits on Void or Month-break, he recovers on the day the Void or the break is filled. Divining a relation's illness for another: one need not look at whether the Subject is strong or weak to know he will be safe, because Output is the spirit of joy, and so long as it is strong, if it moves in the hexagram it is also a sign of good fortune. Juezi adds: in divining a parent's illness, an Output line that holds the Self line and moves controls away the Support of the Resource line; when a wife divines her husband's illness, a moving Output harms the husband; in these cases it is not the spirit of joy, and one must look at it flexibly.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.26** 问卜求神、请医吃药是有道理的：不灵验有两个原因，一是术不精，精了没有不灵验的；二是病要分有根无根，有根的可以救，无根的救不了，就像移栽花木，有根的上面遮盖、下面浇灌，自然慢慢活过来，无根的树、无蒂的花，浇了盖了也没有用。人不懂这个，一次不灵验就不信神和药，是冤枉了它。
  There is reason in consulting the oracle and praying to the spirits, calling a doctor and taking medicine: when it fails there are two causes, one that the art is not refined, for if refined nothing fails, and the other that an illness must be told as rooted or rootless, the rooted being savable and the rootless not, as with moving a flowering plant, where one that has roots is covered above and watered below and revives gradually, while a tree without root or a flower without stem gains nothing from watering and covering. People who do not understand this lose faith in the spirits and medicine at the first failure, and wrong them.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.27** 官鬼这一爻，不是鬼也不是病，是忧神：替儿子占病的，父母爱子的心，没病时都怕他生病担忧，何况已经病了；自己占病的，性命在一呼一吸之间，一个人起不来，全家惊慌，谁不担忧？所以官鬼就是一家人的忧神，能解这份忧的，只有子孙爻。鬼持世或在卦中发动，得到子孙发动来制服它，这个医生可以请，一下手就成功；子孙临在应爻上，是高明的医生，因为子孙克掉的是忧神，我就没有忧愁了。
  The Pressure line is neither ghost nor illness but the spirit of worry: in divining a son's illness, the parents' love for their son is such that even before he is ill they fear illness and worry, and how much more when he is already ill; in divining one's own illness, life hangs on a breath, and when one person cannot rise the whole household is alarmed, and who would not worry? So the Pressure line is the spirit of worry of the whole household, and what can relieve that worry is only the Output line. If Pressure holds the Self line or moves in the hexagram and Output moves to restrain it, this doctor can be called and his hand will succeed; if Output sits on the Other line he is a brilliant doctor, because what Output controls away is the spirit of worry, and then I have no worry.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.29** 应爻当作医生，不宜落在破、空、墓、绝上。自己求医、替六亲求医，都拿应爻当医生。应爻如果空、破、入墓、入绝、休囚衰弱，或者旺相却被日月和动爻冲克，或者动了变成鬼、变成绝、变出回头克，药就不见效。
  The Other line is taken as the doctor and should not be broken, Void, in a tomb or extinct. Whether one seeks a doctor for oneself or for a relation, the Other line is the doctor. If the Other line is Void, broken, in a tomb or extinct, resting, trapped or weak, or is strong but clashed or controlled by the Day, Month or a moving line, or moves and changes into Pressure, into extinction or into a returning control, the medicine has no effect.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.30** 子孙制服鬼，最喜欢旺相、有生扶。子孙临在世爻、应爻上，或者在卦中发动，也要旺相，不受刑、冲、克、害，不遇破、墓、绝、空，药就一定见效；子孙动了变成鬼、变出克，那药就不精；动了变空，到填实空的那天有效；动了变出生、变旺、变进神，药更灵。古人认为子孙动变子孙，药一定杂乱，不对！子孙动变成旺相的子孙，是另外改换了药品，好比仙丹。
  Output restrains Pressure and most wants to be strong and to be generated and supported. If Output sits on the Self or Other line, or moves in the hexagram, it too must be strong, suffer no punishment, clash, control or harm, and meet no break, tomb, extinction or Void, and then the medicine surely takes effect; if Output moves and changes into Pressure or into a control, the medicine is not refined; if it moves and changes into Void, it is effective on the day the Void is filled; if it moves and changes into generation, strength or an Advancing spirit, the medicine is even more effective. The ancients held that Output moving and changing into Output means a mixed medicine, but this is wrong! Output moving and changing into an Output that is strong means that the medicines have been changed, and it is as good as an immortal's elixir.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.31** 医生克用爻，近病就好。自己占病，应爻克世爻，是克制我的病；应爻克用神，也这样断。但是不适宜久病和体弱的人，因为它不光克掉了病，身体也受到伤害。应爻临着兄弟、官鬼而去克用神，以及克世爻的，不管久病近病，一定受它的害。
  The doctor controlling the Subject line: a recent illness heals at once. In divining one's own illness, if the Other line controls the Self line, it restrains my illness; if the Other line controls the Subject, judge the same. It is unsuitable only for one long ill or of weak body, for it controls not only the illness but harms the body too. If the Other line sits on Peer or Pressure and controls the Subject, or controls the Self line, whether the illness is long or recent, harm from it is certain.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.32** 父母爻持世，再好的药也难调治。自己占病，父母持世，药不见功效，宜于静养，远离色欲，平息气恼就可以了；兄弟持世，有子孙在卦中发动的，可以请医生，只嫌鬼旺相、鬼兴起，主吃错药；财爻持世，千万不要误吃油腻甘美的东西。旺相又遇到生扶，能找到良医；休囚又遇到冲克，没有药可以医。
  With the Resource line holding the Self line, even wonderful medicine is hard to prescribe.
