In divining one's own illness, with Resource holding the Self line the medicine shows no effect, and it is fitting to rest quietly, keep away from lust and calm one's anger, and that will do; with Peer holding the Self line, if Output moves in the hexagram a doctor may be called, and the only fear is that Pressure is strong and rising, which means taking the wrong medicine; with the Wealth line holding the Self line, take care not to eat rich food by mistake. If strong and generated and supported, a good doctor can be found; if resting or trapped and clashed or controlled, there is no medicine that can treat him.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.33** 儿子不替父亲占药，妻子不替丈夫卜医。儿子占父亲的病，宜父爻旺相；可是占药、占医，又要拿子孙当用神，父爻旺就会伤子孙，一个爻不能同时当两样用。妻子占丈夫的病，宜官鬼旺相；占医占药，也拿子孙当用神，子孙旺又会伤丈夫，所以不可以替他占医药。
  A son does not divine medicine for his father, nor a wife a doctor for her husband. A son divining his father's illness wants the Resource line strong; but in divining medicine or a doctor Output is taken as the Subject, and a strong Resource line harms Output, and one line cannot serve two uses. A wife divining her husband's illness wants the Pressure line strong; in divining a doctor or medicine Output is also taken as the Subject, and a strong Output harms the husband, so one may not divine medicine or a doctor on his behalf.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.34** 请医生要往子孙所在的方位去请，治病则以应爻来定。卦里子孙爻旺相的，从子孙所在的方位请医生，比如午火子孙，旺相或发动，就请南方的医生。子孙休囚、空、破的，再看应爻旺不旺，如果应爻旺相、又生合用神，就往应爻所在的方位请，比如应爻临申金，名医一定在西南。卦里有一个爻单独发动、而且生用神的，就从这个爻的方位请，并用五行来定医生的姓名：水爻动，姓名带三点水；火爻动，姓名像火；木爻动，带草字头、木字旁；土爻动，姓名里有土；金爻动，姓名里有金。比如占妻子的病，妻爻属水，得申金发动来生它，就请姓钱的医生。
  Call the doctor from the direction of Output; for treatment, the Other line decides. If the Output line in the hexagram is strong, call the doctor from the direction of Output; for example, with Wu Fire Output, strong or moving, call a doctor from the south. If Output is resting, trapped, Void or broken, look at whether the Other line is strong; if the Other line is strong and generates or combines with the Subject, call from the direction of the Other line; for example, with the Other line on Shen Metal, a famous doctor is surely in the south-west. If one line in the hexagram moves alone and generates the Subject, call from the direction of that line, and fix the doctor's name by the five phases: a Water line moving, a name with the water radical; Fire, a name resembling fire; Wood, a name with the grass or wood radical; Earth, a name with earth in it; Metal, a name with metal in it. For example, in divining a wife's illness, if the wife's line is Water and Shen Metal moves and generates it, call a doctor with the surname Qian.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.36** 医生自己占要不要去治，和替别人占卜差不多：世爻是自己，应爻是病人。世爻旺相，子孙或财爻持世，世爻生应爻，世动变进神、变回头生，日月和动爻临着财星或子孙来生世爻，都主手到病除。近病：应爻值旬空，或动了变空、变回头生、变旺、变退，或日月、动爻生应爻，以及卦逢六冲、卦变六冲的，都该赶快去救，别让别人先到而成了功。久病：应爻旺相，或遇到日月、动爻来生，或动了变生、变旺，应爻临子孙、临财爻，动而生世爻的，虽然是久病，我一定能除根。除了这些，都不要去治，白白损了名声。
  A doctor divining for himself whether to go and treat is much like divining for another: the Self line is oneself and the Other line is the patient. If the Self line is strong, Output or Wealth holds the Self line, the Self line generates the Other line, the Self line moves and changes into an Advancing spirit or into a returning generation, or the Day, Month or a moving line sits on the Wealth star or Output and generates the Self line, all mean that the hand arrives and the illness is gone. For a recent illness: if the Other line falls into Void, or moves and changes into Void, a returning generation, strength or retreat, or the Day, Month or a moving line generates the Other line, or the hexagram is Six-Clash or changes into Six-Clash, one should hurry to the rescue and not let others arrive first and succeed. For a long illness: if the Other line is strong, or is generated by the Day, Month or a moving line, or moves and changes into generation or strength, or sits on Output or Wealth, or moves and generates the Self line, then though the illness is long I shall surely remove the root. Apart from these, do not go to treat, for it only harms one's reputation.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.37** 占药能不能服，拿子孙当用神。医生治垂危的病、想用某味药，可以起死回生，又怕一有闪失；泛海行船、长途旅行没有地方找医生、有人传来奇方；半夜来不及请医、手边的古方或家里的药散确实对这个病却不敢擅用，都要占。只要子孙持世或发动，服了立刻可以解忧，必定见效。子孙不管是金木水火土哪一行，旺相或发动就是喜悦的神；不可以又因为金临子孙就用针、火属子孙就用灸。
  To divine whether a medicine may be taken, take Output as the Subject. It should be divined when a doctor treating a dying patient wants a certain medicine that may bring him back from death, and fears one failure; when one is crossing the sea or on a long journey with nowhere to find a doctor and someone passes on a rare prescription; when in the depth of night there is no time to call a doctor and the old prescriptions or powders kept at home truly fit the illness but one does not dare use them on one's own authority. If Output holds the Self line or moves, taking it at once relieves the worry, and it will surely work. Whatever phase Output is, metal, wood, water, fire or earth, so long as it is strong or moving it is the spirit of joy; one must not take Metal on Output to mean the needle, or Fire as Output to mean moxibustion.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.38** 前面几篇都说子孙持世，病就马上好，但一定要子孙不受克才行，后来的人不可以不知道。比如有人生了杨梅疮，占这位医生好不好：虽然是子孙持世、应爻是医生，但初爻巳火发动克申金，又有卯木生火，世爻虽旺，巳日一定添灾；他不听，还是请了这位医生，卯日、辰日服药还平安，巳日忽然病症变了，全身疼痛难以忍受。
  The earlier passages all say that with Output holding the Self line the illness heals at once, but it is so only when Output is not controlled, which later people must know. For example, a man with syphilitic sores divined whether a certain doctor was a good one: though Output held the Self line and the Other line was the doctor, the first line, Si Fire, moved and controlled Shen Metal, with Mao Wood also generating Fire, so that though the Self line was strong there would be added trouble on the Si day; he did not listen and called the doctor, and on the Mao and Chen days the medicine kept him safe, but on the Si day the symptoms suddenly changed and pain all over the body was unbearable.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.40** 子孙旺相又发动的，不吃药就好，并不是教人拿这个子孙当治病的药；前辈贤人常常看到子孙旺动，占病病好、占医医良，就把子孙爻当成治病的药，金生子孙就断用针，火持子孙就断用灸，不知道子孙旺相病自己就好了，哪里用得着针灸，子孙落空被破，针灸又有什么益处。各种书又说火鬼忌热、水鬼忌寒，水鬼在生旺之地要用大热的药，火鬼旺相宜用大寒的药去攻；李我平不知道他们从哪里看出来的：假使用神属土、儿子占父亲的病，父爻临土，火鬼发动是生用爻的元神，怎么能用大寒的药去治元神？妻子占丈夫的病，鬼是用神，丈夫临水鬼，又怎么能用大热的药去攻用神？死板地照大寒大热去断，坑害了不知多少性命。
  Where Output is strong and moving, one recovers without medicine, and this does not teach us to take that Output as the medicine that treats the illness; the worthies of old often saw Output strong and moving, found that in divining illness the illness healed and in divining a doctor the doctor was good, and so took the Output line as the medicine, judging the needle where Metal generates Output and moxibustion where Fire holds Output, without realising that if Output is strong the illness heals of itself, so what need is there of needle and moxa, and if Output is Void or broken what good would they do. The books also say that a Fire Pressure fears heat and a Water Pressure fears cold, that a Water Pressure in a place of birth or strength must be treated with very hot medicine and a strong Fire Pressure attacked with very cold; Li Woping does not know where they saw this: suppose the Subject is Earth, as when a son divines his father's illness and the Resource line sits on Earth, a moving Fire Pressure is the Support that generates the Subject line, and how could one treat the Support with very cold medicine? When a wife divines her husband's illness, Pressure is the Subject, and if the husband sits on a Water Pressure, how could one attack the Subject with very hot medicine? To judge rigidly by very cold and very hot has sunk who knows how many lives in the pit.

## 神祟(Spirits and curses: prayer, vows and what to sacrifice)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/疾病/神祟.md`

- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.37** 怎么知道这家的人多做噩梦：螣蛇带着鬼来持世；出鬼怪：螣蛇、白虎临在门上；有人投水：玄武入水，煞神临在鬼上；有人上吊：螣蛇、木鬼落在世爻上；要有孝服（丧事）来：卜筮正宗说是白虎交重（发动）、排在鬼上，卜筮大全说是丧门、吊客两颗星排在鬼上。
  How to know that a household is troubled by dreams: the Coiling Snake carrying the ghost holds the Self position; that ghosts and strange things appear: the Coiling Snake and White Tiger sit at the door; that someone will throw himself into water: the Dark Tortoise enters Water and a killing spirit sits on the ghost; that someone will hang himself: the Coiling Snake and a Wood ghost sit on the Self line; that mourning dress will come: in the Bushi Zhengzong the White Tiger moves and is set upon the ghost, in the Bushi Daquan the stars Mourning Gate and Guest of Condolence are set upon the ghost.
- **LY.ILL.EL.29** 以官鬼和动爻为主，以五行和六兽（六神）为凭。凡是论神灵的职司，必须凭官鬼，只要不逢旬空，或旺或衰都是作祟，只要出现在卦上，不论动静，一概按神仙来推。先看鬼落在哪一行，再察官鬼临的是哪个六兽，再查病的来源是缓是急，就知道是什么祟作怪。注说：如果占神灵的职司，要推官鬼；鬼如果空亡或不上卦，一定没有鬼祟，不可妄说；鬼如果不空，不论衰旺动静，都作为神灵的职司；如果发动或持世，神力狂暴，冒犯了应当赶快祷告；还要看鬼落在哪一爻、哪个六兽上，才说是什么鬼、什么神，六兽和五行列在后面。
  The Pressure line and the moving lines are the main lines, and the five phases and the six beasts (the six spirits) are the ground. In judging the offices of the spirits one must rely on the Pressure line; so long as it does not fall on Void, strong or weak it makes haunting, and so long as it appears in the hexagram, moving or still, it is in all cases reckoned as an immortal spirit. See first which phase the ghost falls on, then examine which of the six beasts the Pressure line sits on, then check whether the source of the illness is slow or urgent, and one knows what haunting is doing the harm. The commentary says: if divining the office of a spirit, work out the Pressure line; if the ghost is Void or absent from the hexagram, there is surely no haunting by a ghost, and one must not speak at random; if the ghost is not Void, whether weak or strong, moving or still, it stands for a spirit's office; if it moves or holds the Self, the spirit's power is fierce and wild, and if offended one should pray at once; look also at which line and which beast the ghost sits on, and only then say which ghost and which spirit; the six beasts and five phases are set out below.
- **LY.ILL.EL.30** 金鬼，是死在刀下的魂，喘咳、横死的鬼：症状缓的，是关公、总管；症状急的，是丧部、伤司。带青龙，是汉寿亭侯（关公）；朱雀，是金都元帅；勾陈、螣蛇，是七煞；白虎，是丧殃；玄武，是曹堂、西府。把它送走，病体就安康。注说：金鬼，主刀枪伤死的鬼、喘咳的鬼、横死的鬼；症状缓的，宜向关爷和总管祷告；病急的，必须酬谢丧煞和伤神；带青龙，是云长公（关公）有所妨碍；临朱雀，是金元七作祟为灾；带勾陈、螣蛇，是七煞土；带白虎，是丧煞和伤司；带玄武，是水伤、水道一类。
  Metal is the soul beneath the blade and the ghost of asthma-cough and violent death: if mild, Guan Gong and the Chief Official; if severe, the Mourning Department and the Injuring Office. With the Azure Dragon, the Marquis of Hanshou; the Vermilion Bird is the Golden Capital Marshal; Hook Earth and the Coiling Snake are the Seven Killings; the White Tiger is Mourning Calamity; the Dark Tortoise is the Cao Hall and the Western Bureau. Send it away, and the sick body is well. The commentary says: a Metal ghost governs ghosts of death by blade and spear, ghosts of asthma-cough and ghosts of violent death; if the symptoms are mild, pray to Lord Guan and the Chief Official; if the illness is urgent, one must make offering to the Mourning Killing and the Injuring spirit; with the Azure Dragon, it is an obstruction from Lord Yunchang; on the Vermilion Bird, Lord Jin Yuanqi is making the calamity; with Hook Earth and the Coiling Snake, it is the Seven Killings Earth; with the White Tiger, it is the Mourning Killing and the Injuring Office; with the Dark Tortoise, it is the water injury and water-way sort.
- **LY.ILL.EL.31** 木鬼，是被杖责的魂，疯病、上吊的鬼：症状缓的，是山神、五圣；症状急的，是东兵（东岳）、家堂。白虎同在，就在门外酬谢伤神，用锣鼓追送；青龙同位，就在堂前酬还原愿，用笙萧；勾陈，是九良星煞；螣蛇，是树圣、山神；朱雀、玄武，是草野三郎，祭祀时要提防，灾非都落在你身上。注说：木鬼，主受刑责、戴枷锁的鬼、疯病鬼、上吊死的鬼；症状缓的，必是冒犯了山神、土地和五圣尊神；症状急的，必是干犯了东岳和家堂的众神；带白虎，赶快酬谢大小伤司；带青龙，宜酬还枷锁的愿，以及祷告喜庆的神；带勾陈，是冒犯了九良星；土带螣蛇，如果不是山神，一定是树上的神，又是作犯土；带朱雀或玄武，都是草野三郎。
  Wood is the soul of one beaten with the rod, the ghost of madness and of hanging from a beam: if mild, the Mountain God and the Five Sages; if severe, the Eastern Soldiers (the Eastern Peak) and the household shrine. With the White Tiger in the same place, thank the Injuring spirit outside the gate and send it off with gongs and drums; with the Azure Dragon in the same place, redeem the vow in front of the hall with pipes and flutes; Hook Earth is the Nine Good Stars and the killing stars; the Coiling Snake is the Tree Sage or Mountain God; the Vermilion Bird and the Dark Tortoise are the Third Lord of the wilds, and in sacrificing be careful, for calamity and blame will all fall on you. The commentary says: a Wood ghost governs ghosts of punishment, the cangue and fetters, ghosts of madness and ghosts of hanging; if the symptoms are mild, it is surely from offending the Mountain God, the Earth God and the honoured Five Sages; if urgent, surely from offending the Eastern Peak and the gods of the household shrine; with the White Tiger, quickly make offering to the greater and lesser Injuring Offices; with the Azure Dragon, redeem the vow of the cangue and pray to the spirits of festivity; with Hook Earth, there is an offence against the Nine Good Stars; Earth with the Coiling Snake, if not the Mountain God, must be the spirit in a tree, and is also the offence-making earth; with the Vermilion Bird or the Dark Tortoise, it is the Third Lord of the wilds.
- **LY.ILL.EL.32** 水鬼，是投河、奔井的魂，服卤、腰疼的鬼：症状缓的，是水仙、施相；症状急的，是河太、金龙。阳龙（阳的青龙），断作云台法主，也是冒犯了萧公；阴龙，推作南海慈尊，又冲犯了杜氏；玄武，是佑圣真君；朱雀，恐怕是对江河许过愿；遇到勾陈、螣蛇，断作坑厕；逢白虎，论作水伤。注说：水鬼，主淹死的鬼、腰疼的鬼、服卤死的鬼；症状缓的，是水仙五圣和镇海施相公；症状急的，是冒犯了水中的河太、或曹三和金龙四大王；阳鬼值青龙，是三官大帝和五圣的神；阴鬼值青龙，是观世音和杜氏夫人；鬼带玄武，是北极真君；带朱雀，是在水池上许的愿心；带勾陈或螣蛇，就说是水口上冒犯的；如果水鬼变出兄弟、或水的兄弟变出鬼、又带勾陈螣蛇的，就是坑厕的土神；鬼临白虎，必须向水部的伤司求。
  Water is the soul of one who threw himself into a river or ran to a well, the ghost of one who drank brine ('you' in the text, for 'lu') or of back pain: if mild, the Water Immortal and Minister Shi; if severe, He Tai and the Golden Dragon. A yang Dragon is judged the Master of the Yuntai Rite, and also offends Lord Xiao; a yin Dragon is worked out as the Compassionate Honoured One of the Southern Sea, and also clashes with Lady Du; the Dark Tortoise is the True Lord Yousheng; the Vermilion Bird: beware a vow made to river or lake; meeting Hook Earth and the Coiling Snake, judge it the pit and privy; meeting the White Tiger, judge it the water injury. The commentary says: a Water ghost governs ghosts of drowning, ghosts of back pain and ghosts of death by drinking brine; if the symptoms are mild, it is the Water (the text says Fire) Immortal and the Five Sages, and the Sea-Pacifying Lord Shi; if urgent, it is an offence against He Tai in the water, or Cao San and the Golden Dragon Fourth Great King; a yang ghost on the Azure Dragon is the Three Officials the Great Emperors and the spirits of the Five Sages; a yin ghost on the Azure Dragon is Guanyin and Lady Du; a ghost with the Dark Tortoise is the True Lord of the North Pole; with the Vermilion Bird, a vow made by a pool; with Hook Earth or the Coiling Snake, say an offence at a water mouth; if the Water ghost changes into Peer, or a Water Peer changes into a ghost and carries Hook Earth and the Coiling Snake, it is the earth spirit of the pit and privy; a ghost on the White Tiger must seek the Injuring Office of the Water Department.
- **LY.ILL.EL.33** 火鬼，是毒疮、痨病的魂，带血、焚烧的鬼：症状缓的，是东厨、香愿；症状急的，是陆相、华山。青龙，冒犯了五福星；勾陈、螣蛇，动了三煞的土；白虎，是玄坛再加上横死的鬼；玄武，是南堂和水神一起；朱雀，是华光、司命，并且酬还口愿才安宁。注说：火鬼，主疮毒鬼、痨病鬼、带血鬼、烧死的鬼、心疼的鬼；症状缓的，宜酬谢灶神和香愿；症状急的，也有轻重之分：轻病宜酬陆引，陆相就是南堂；重病宜拜华山，华山就是五福大神；带青龙也是五福；带勾陈或螣蛇都是三煞土；带白虎是赵玄坛和痴癫鬼；带玄武是南堂和水神；带朱雀是华光，华光就是五显灵官，又是司命，就是灶神。
  Fire is the soul of poisonous sores and consumption, the ghost of bleeding and of burning: if mild, the Eastern Kitchen and the incense vow; if severe, Minister Lu and Huashan. The Azure Dragon offends the Five Blessings star; Hook Earth and the Coiling Snake move the earth of the Three Killings; the White Tiger, the Dark Altar, and a violent-death ghost added; the Dark Tortoise, the Southern Hall and the water spirit together; the Vermilion Bird, Huaguang and the Director of Fate, and the spoken vow redeemed, then there is peace. The commentary says: a Fire ghost governs ghosts of sores and poison, ghosts of consumption, ghosts of bleeding, ghosts of burning to death and ghosts of heart pain; if the symptoms are mild, thank the Kitchen God and the incense vow; if urgent, there are still degrees: a light illness suits an offering to Lu Yin, Minister Lu being the Southern Hall; a heavy illness suits worship at Huashan, Huashan being the Great God of the Five Blessings; with the Azure Dragon it is also the Five Blessings; with Hook Earth or the Coiling Snake it is the Three Killings Earth; with the White Tiger it is Zhao Xuantan and a ghost of madness; with the Dark Tortoise it is the Southern Hall and a water spirit; with the Vermilion Bird it is Huaguang, and Huaguang is the Five Manifest Spirit Officials and also the Director of Fate, who is the Kitchen God.
- **LY.ILL.EL.34** 土鬼，是瘟疫的魂，膨胀、虚黄的鬼：症状缓的，是庙神、土府；症状急的，是贤圣、城隍。青龙，是素土；勾陈，叫土皇；白虎，是金神，忌它；玄武，是坑厕的妨害；螣蛇，应当作犯，并且求本境的神；朱雀，合于天曹，并且谢飞游的土。注说：土鬼，主瘟疫鬼、膨胀鬼、黄病鬼；症状缓的，是庙里的神、大的是土神；症状急的，是五方贤圣、又是城隍；带青龙，是正土，宜念素诰；带勾陈，是土皇，宜中奏；带白虎，是金神，就是七煞；带玄武，是水口上的作犯，如果鬼变出兄弟、或兄弟变出鬼，就是坑厕；土带螣蛇是作犯，土又是螣蛇，也是当地的杀神祠；带朱雀是飞土；如果在乾、兑两卦，就是天曹；即使不在乾、兑卦中，如果带天咒、地咒或负结煞，也是天曹土。天咒煞说：正月二月鼠来，三月四月酉，五月六月马头，七月猴走，八月鸡，九月犬，十月逢猪，子、兔、丑、鼠，是天咒；地咒煞从正月起在卯上顺行十二位；负结煞说：正月二月猪亏，三月四月牛，五月六月其星向兔游，七月八月蛇宫，九月十月未，十一月十二月酉中求。
  Earth is the soul of epidemic, the ghost of swelling and pale-yellow emptiness: if mild, the temple gods and the earth mansion; if severe, the sages and the City God. The Azure Dragon is the plain Earth; Hook Earth is called the Earth Emperor; the White Tiger is the Golden Spirit, which is to be avoided; the Dark Tortoise is the hindrance of the pit and privy; the Coiling Snake should be the offence-maker, and one seeks the god of the local region; the Vermilion Bird accords with the Heavenly Bureau, and one thanks the Flying-Wandering Earth. The commentary says: an Earth ghost governs ghosts of epidemic, ghosts of swelling and ghosts of jaundice; if the symptoms are mild, it is a god within a temple, or an earth god of greater rank; if urgent, it is the sages of the five quarters and also the City God; with the Azure Dragon, it is the right Earth, and a plain prayer is suitable; with Hook Earth, it is the Earth Emperor, and a central memorial is suitable; with the White Tiger, it is the Golden Spirit, that is, the Seven Killings; with the Dark Tortoise, it is an offence at a water mouth, and if the ghost changes into Peer or Peer changes into a ghost, it is the pit and privy; Earth with the Coiling Snake is the offence-maker, and Earth is also the Coiling Snake, which is also the local killing-god shrine; with the Vermilion Bird it is the flying earth; if it is in the Qian or Dui hexagram it is the Heavenly Bureau, and even if it is not in Qian or Dui, if it carries the Heavenly Curse, the Earth Curse or the Burden-Knot killing it is also the Heavenly Bureau earth. The Heavenly Curse killing says: 'in the first and second months the Rat comes, third and fourth the You, fifth and sixth the Horse's head, seventh the Monkey runs, eighth the Rooster, ninth the Dog, tenth meets the Pig, Zi, Rabbit, Chou, Rat': this is the Heavenly Curse; the Earth Curse killing runs forward twelve places from Mao in the first month; the Burden-Knot killing says: 'first and second the Pig is deficient, third and fourth the Ox, fifth and sixth its star wanders toward the Rabbit, seventh and eighth the Snake's palace, ninth and tenth Wei, eleventh and twelfth seek in You'.
- **LY.ILL.EL.35** 看鬼落在哪一卦、哪一爻发动，就知道在那里冒犯了什么神、什么煞，因而牵连上身：官鬼在坎位，是北方来侵，或是兴动水口；鬼到离宫，是南向的妨碍，或是在灶前动工；艮主山林，或是遭遇东北；坤成坟墓，或是值西南；乾是西北，兑是西方，或是冒犯天曹、修寺观；巽是东南；震是东方，或是砍伐树林并起造。六爻的鬼，必是造墙作墓；五爻的官，必是砌路修街；四爻，断门栏，或是在檐下动工；三爻，推房内，或是动犯桥梁；二爻，是修厨房、作灶；初爻，是穿井、开沟；鬼在世爻上，是本宅动了工；在应爻上，是对门在挖掘；临门的爻，是开窗不便；变出兄弟，是挖坑、建造不通。注说：以上说的，都是把上面的鬼逐一说明白，不必再注。
  Look at which trigram and which line the ghost moves in, to know where a god or a killing was offended and thereby brought on the body: the Pressure line in the Kan place means encroachment from the north, or works at a water mouth; a ghost at the Li palace means obstruction facing south, or works in front of the stove; Gen governs mountain and forest, or meets the northeast; Kun makes graves, or falls southwest; Qian is northwest and Dui is west, or an offence against the Heavenly Bureau in repairing a temple or abbey; Xun is southeast; Zhen is east, or felling woods and building. A ghost on the sixth line surely means building a wall or making a grave; the Pressure line on the fifth surely means paving a road or repairing a street; the fourth, judge gates and railings, or works under the eaves; the third, work out the inside of the house, or works offending bridges and beams; the second is repairing the kitchen or making a stove; the first is digging a well or opening a ditch; on the Self line, works on one's own house; on the Other line, digging opposite the door; on the door line, opening a window is inconvenient; changing into Peer, a pit or structure is blocked. The commentary says: all of the above sets the ghosts out one by one, and there is no need for further notes.
- **LY.ILL.EL.36** 亥是天门，和张壬作扰害；带青龙的象，宜叩拜三元；子是北斗，加上河伯作为灾殃；加了玄武的爻，应当酬谢圣帝；丑，是被牛顶撞的魂；寅，是被虎伤的鬼；卯，要禳解东岳；辰，要酬谢龙王；巳，推为火德尊星、被蛇所伤的鬼；午，断为金枪教主、被马踏的魂；未，叫伽蓝；申，说是元帅；酉，叫雌雄二煞；戌，遇上恶犬伤人。注说：亥鬼是张壬，就是祠山大帝；外鬼带青龙，是三官大帝；内鬼带青龙，是水仙五圣；子鬼是北斗、又是河伯水官，在外带玄武，是驿帝，在内带玄武，是水伤；丑鬼是土神，又是土伤之鬼；寅鬼是东岳，又是虎伤之鬼；卯鬼也是东岳；辰鬼是土神，又是龙王；巳鬼是火德星君和蚕室，又是蛇伤之鬼；午鬼是金枪教主，就是五显灵官，以及马伤之鬼；未鬼是土神，又是伽蓝土地；申鬼是元帅将军的职司和伤司，又有寺观中所冒犯的神；酉鬼是佛象和丧煞，又是少女；戌鬼是土神，又是犬伤之鬼。
  Hai is the Heavenly Gate and the harm done by Zhang Ren; with the image of the Azure Dragon, it is proper to appeal to the Three Primes; Zi is the Northern Dipper together with the River Count, as calamity; a line with the Dark Tortoise added should make offering to the Holy Emperor; Chou is the soul gored by an ox; Yin is the ghost wounded by a tiger; Mao: exorcise the Eastern Peak; Chen: thank the Dragon King; Si: work out the honoured Fire Virtue star and the ghost wounded by a snake; Wu: judge the Golden Spear Master and the soul trampled by a horse; Wei is called the Sangharama; Shen is said to be the Marshal; You is named the two killings, male and female; Xu meets a fierce dog wounding a person. The commentary says: the Hai ghost is Zhang Ren, that is, the Great Emperor of Ci Mountain; an outer ghost with the Azure Dragon is the Three Officials the Great Emperors; an inner ghost with the Azure Dragon is the Water Immortal and the Five Sages; a Zi ghost is the Northern Dipper and also the River Count and Water Official, and with the Dark Tortoise outside it is the Post Emperor, and inside it is the water injury; a Chou ghost is an earth spirit and also a ghost wounded by earth; a Yin ghost is the Eastern Peak and also a ghost wounded by a tiger; a Mao ghost is also the Eastern Peak; a Chen ghost is an earth spirit and also the Dragon King; a Si ghost is the Fire Virtue Star Lord and the silkworm house, and also a ghost wounded by a snake; a Wu ghost is the Golden Spear Master, that is, the Five Manifest Spirit Officials, and a ghost wounded by a horse; a Wei ghost is an earth spirit and also the Sangharama Earth God; a Shen ghost is the office of marshals and generals and the Injuring Office, and also a god offended in a temple or abbey; a You ghost is a Buddha image and the Mourning Killing, and also a young woman; a Xu ghost is an earth spirit and also a ghost wounded by a dog.
- **LY.ILL.EL.37** 六爻，是上仓的至圣、坟墓的神；五爻，是中界的至尊、路途的鬼；四爻，推为檐外伤、朝门前的魍魉；三爻断，鬼在六爻，是上苍的素原、以及坟墓土神、或远方的鬼；鬼在五爻，是中界至尊，就是东岳，又是栏路五圣、五路大神、或倒在路上的鬼；鬼在四爻，是大小伤神、以及右仪门的将军、又是门前的鬼；鬼在三爻，是家堂和郡王，就是府城隍，又是床婆、弟兄的鬼，以及房内的鬼、桥上的鬼；鬼在二爻，是县城隍和灶神，又是夫妻等的鬼、厨下的鬼；鬼在初爻，是土地和井泉童子、以及井前的鬼，如果没有井，就是土鬼。
  The sixth line is the supreme sage of the upper granary and the god of graves; the fifth is the supreme honoured one of the middle realm and the ghost of the road; the fourth is worked out as an injury beyond the eaves and the goblins before the court gate; the third judge: a ghost on the sixth line is the plain vow of the upper heaven, the earth god of graves, or a ghost from a far place; a ghost on the fifth line is the supreme honoured one of the middle realm, that is, the Eastern Peak, and also the Five Sages of the barrier road, the Great God of the Five Roads, or a ghost fallen on the road; a ghost on the fourth line is the greater and lesser Injuring gods, and the general at the right ceremonial gate, and also a ghost before the door; a ghost on the third line is the household shrine and the Prince of the Commandery, that is, the City God of the prefecture, and also the Bed Mother and a ghost of brothers, and a ghost within the room and a ghost on the bridge; a ghost on the second line is the City God of the county and the Kitchen God, and also a ghost of husband and wife and a ghost beneath the kitchen; a ghost on the first line is the Earth God and the Well-and-Spring Boy, and a ghost before the well, and if there is no well it is an earth ghost.
- **LY.ILL.EL.38** 官鬼临在世爻上，要注明六象的神；鬼落在空亡中，不要断作哪一份殃祟。注说：鬼在初爻持世，是家堂作祸；鬼在二爻持世，上气为实，并且是社坛作祟；鬼在三爻持世，是冒犯了桥道中的鬼，如果不是桥道，就是园中花木的精怪；鬼在四爻持世，是冒犯了五道，也有师王佛实的灾；鬼在五爻持世，原文说没有（可说的）不言；鬼在六爻持世，是山神为害，以及星宿降灾；六爻里鬼虽然持世，落空了就不说。
  The Pressure line on the Self line: note the spirits of the six images; a ghost in Void: do not judge a haunting of any part of calamity. The commentary says: a ghost on the first line holding the Self is the household shrine making trouble; a ghost on the second holding the Self, the upper qi is real, and the altar of the soil haunts; a ghost on the third holding the Self offends the ghost of a bridge or road, and if not a bridge or road, it is the spirit of garden flowers and trees; a ghost on the fourth holding the Self offends the Five Roads, and there is also calamity from teachers, kings and Buddhas (the text says 'shi'); with the fifth, if there is no ghost, nothing is said; a ghost on the sixth holding the Self is the Mountain God doing harm and stars descending calamity; though the ghost in the six lines holds the Self, if it is Void nothing is said.
- **LY.ILL.EL.39** 青龙、财爻、子孙变出官爻，是福神来照应；兑宫、朱雀、文书变成鬼象，是先前许的愿在催。注说：青龙如果临财、玄武临福（子孙），变出官爻，必是福神见咎；福神，大的是五福和茶延，小的是五圣和五路；凡是判断鬼神，必须审察病源的轻重，重的就是大神，轻的就是小祟；再看朱雀临父母变出鬼爻，必是先年许下的愿；如果落在兑卦，又是冒犯了天曹。
  The Azure Dragon, Wealth and Output changing into a Pressure line: the fortune spirit shines on it; Dui, the Vermilion Bird and the writing image becoming a ghost image: the earlier vow presses. The commentary says: if the Azure Dragon sits on Wealth and the Dark Tortoise on Output (the fortune spirit) and it changes into a Pressure line, it is surely Output (the fortune spirit) showing fault; the fortune spirit, at the great end, is the Five Blessings and the Tea Feast, and at the small end the Five Sages and the Five Roads; in judging ghosts and spirits one must examine the lightness or weight of the source of the illness, the heavy being a great god and the light a small haunting; look further at the Vermilion Bird on the Resource line changing into a Pressure line, which is surely a vow made in a former year; if it sits in the Dui hexagram, it is again an offence against the Heavenly Bureau.
- **LY.ILL.EL.40** 父母爻在内发动，才断祠堂里的宗相；父母爻在外发动，可以说是外族的高亲；财爻在内宫动，必是妻子的魂、妾的魄；妻财在外卦摇动，不是奴仆就是情人；兄弟发动，是手足之亲、相知的人；子孙发动，是儿孙的鬼，僧道的灵。注说：父爻在内卦以及在亲宫动的，就说是本宗的尊长；父爻居外卦以及在他宫动的，就说是外姓的尊亲；财爻临内卦或在亲宫动的，必是先亡的妻妾；财爻在他宫以及外卦动的，如果不是奴仆，必是恩爱的情人；兄弟动的，或是兄弟、或是姨妹、或是朋友的魂；子孙动的，是儿女、是侄、是女婿，又是僧道的魂。
  The Resource line moving inside is judged the ancestral image of the ancestral hall; the Resource line moving outside may be called a high relative of another clan; Wealth moving in the inner palace is surely a wife's spirit and a concubine's soul; Wealth stirring in the outer trigram is either a servant or a lover; Peer thriving is a kin of hand and foot, an intimate; Output moving is the ghost of sons and grandsons, the spirit of a monk or Daoist. The commentary says: the Resource line moving in the inner trigram or in a kin palace is called an elder of one's own line; the Resource line in the outer trigram or moving in another palace is a revered kinsman of another surname; Wealth in the inner trigram or moving in a kin palace is surely a wife or concubine who died earlier; Wealth in another palace or moving in the outer trigram, if not a servant, is surely a loving sweetheart; when Peer moves, it is the spirit of a brother, or an aunt or sister, or a friend; when Output moves, it is a son or daughter, a nephew, a son-in-law, and also the spirit of a monk or Daoist.
- **LY.ILL.EL.41** 卦里有动爻，病里就有鬼魅：比如三爻动，就是三个魂，四个象兴动就是四个鬼。要定阴阳，重、单是男，交、折是女；要分方向，卯动是东方，酉动是西方。注说：凡是推儿官，专看动爻，一爻动就是一个魂，两爻动就是两个鬼；想分男女，要看阴阳，重的是男，交的是女；想知道鬼吃荤还是吃素，只有子孙和青龙爻动的，都宜用素祭，其余的爻动，一概荤素都合；想知道鬼在哪个方位，就看动在哪一象：比如子爻动，是正北方的鬼；丑爻动，是东北方的鬼；其余照这样推。
  Where the hexagram has moving lines, the illness has ghosts and sprites: for example, if the third line moves, there are three souls, and if four images stir, four ghosts. To fix yin and yang, the double and the single are male, the crossed and the broken are female; to divide the direction, a moving Mao is east and a moving You is west. The commentary says: in working out a child-official, look only at the moving lines: one line moving is one soul, two lines moving are two ghosts; to divide male and female, look at yin and yang, the double being male and the crossed female; to know whether the ghost eats meat or vegetables, only when Output or the Azure Dragon line moves is a vegetarian offering suitable, and when any other line moves, both meat and vegetarian suit; to know in which direction the ghost is, look at which image it moves in: for example, a moving Zi line is a ghost of the due north, and a moving Chou line a ghost of the northeast; the rest follow this.
- **LY.ILL.EL.43** 仔细查看是哪个象临在官鬼上，才议论是哪位神在作祟，这样请祷就没有福不到的，祈禳就没有祸不消的。现在作这一篇，恳切是为了却灾、度人性命，又恐怕人乱判断，常常担心人好杀生而白费钱财，万万不可轻易说，一样一样都要细细论，把六兽端正地配上五行来决断诸神，终究没有一个错误，详审它的确实，才判得没有私心。
  Examine carefully which image sits on the Pressure line, and only then discuss which god is haunting; so prayer brings blessing everywhere and exorcism removes calamity everywhere. This chapter is written earnestly to ward off calamity and save lives, and for fear that people judge at random, always worrying that they are fond of killing and waste money, one must by no means speak lightly; each point must be discussed in detail, matching the six beasts correctly with the five phases to decide the spirits, so that in the end there is not one error, and its certainty is examined carefully, and only then is the judgment without partiality.
- **LY.ILL.EL.44** 官鬼发动，看它落在哪一宫，就知道是哪一路的神鬼。乾宫：西北方来，是误犯了寺观或庙里的神司，或者旧时许下的天灯、香愿、经卷、斗斋这一类愿，是已经去世的父辈和年老过世的人的魂，是头风的鬼。坎宫：祸在北方，是水路的神司，还有家里的中男在作耗，也有耳聋的鬼、溺死的魂；坎在外三爻时又是北斗，再加玄武也当作玄天上帝。艮宫：东北方来，是冒犯了土神、五圣、山神、土地，也有家里的少男，以及臂上生疮、手折、指烂、烂鼻的鬼。震宫：祸在东方，宜向九天（雷祀大帝）叩祷，又犯三茅真君、东岳尊神和树头的神灵，也有家里的长男作祟，或是天嗔，或是被打死兼折足、驾舟的鬼。
  When the Pressure line moves, look at which palace it falls in, to know which kind of god or ghost it is. Qian: it comes from the northwest, and one has wrongly offended a temple or abbey or the gods and offices within a temple, or an old vow of a heaven lamp, incense, scriptures or a Dipper fast; it is the soul of a departed father or of an aged person who has died, the ghost of head-wind (chronic headache). Kan: the misfortune lies in the north; it is the gods and offices of the waterways, with the middle son of the household making trouble, and also ghosts of the deaf and souls of the drowned; when Kan is in the outer three lines it is also the Northern Dipper, and with the Dark Tortoise added it is also counted as the Supreme Emperor of the Mysterious Heaven. Gen: it comes from the northeast, offending the Earth spirit, the Five Sages, the Mountain God and the Earth God, and there is also the youngest son of the household and ghosts of arm sores, broken hands, rotten fingers and rotten noses. Zhen: the misfortune lies in the east, and it is fitting to pray to the Nine Heavens (the Great Emperor of the Thunder Rites); it also offends the True Lord of the Three Mao, the honoured god of the Eastern Peak and the sacred spirits of the treetops, and there is the eldest son as haunting, or heaven's wrath, or ghosts beaten to death, with broken legs, or who piloted boats.
- **LY.ILL.EL.45** 巽宫：病患在东南，是冒犯了镇海的施相公、花园树木的神道，以及长女和上吊死的、疯疾的、腿折的、驼背的鬼。离宫：祸从南方起，是火部的诸神——南斗六司、火德星君、五显灵官、香头灶神这一类，还有中女以及眼盲、火烧这类的鬼，要赶紧酬愿、焚香。坤宫：西南方来，是冒犯了土皇使人不安，或者是坟墓上的缘故，和已故的母亲、年老的妇人，以及面色虚黄、腹胀如鼓的鬼。兑宫：祸落在正西，是许过佛、天的愿，也犯了伤神和少女，以及缺唇的、师巫这类的鬼；再加朱雀发动，不是自己发过誓、赌过咒，就是别人在天曹那里咒了他。
  Xun: the illness lies in the southeast, and it is an offence against Lord Shi who pacifies the sea, the spirits of garden trees, and the eldest daughter and ghosts of the hanged, of madness, of broken legs and of bent backs. Li: the misfortune rises in the south, and it is the gods of the Fire Department, the likes of the Six Departments of the Southern Dipper, the Fire Virtue Star Lord, the Five Manifest Spirit Officials and the incense head and Kitchen God, and also the middle daughter and ghosts of blindness and burning, and the vow should be redeemed quickly and incense burned. Kun: it comes from the southwest, and it is an offence against the Earth Emperor that brings unrest, or a matter of graves, together with the late mother and old women and ghosts of pale-yellow emptiness and drum-like swelling. Dui: the misfortune stains the due west, and it is a vow made to Buddha or Heaven, and it also offends the injuring spirit and the youngest daughter and ghosts of the harelipped, teachers and shamans; and if the Vermilion Bird moves too, then if it is not one's own sworn oath it is someone else cursing at the Heavenly Bureau.
- **LY.ILL.EL.46** 官鬼落在哪一行的爻上，就是哪一路的神鬼。金爻：是煞，还有西伤（就是五道）；总管、丧神、七煞；张相（就是六五相公）和金神七煞、金元七总管、寺里的金刚、自刎的鬼、锁条的愿；叶神（就是九卓）、钟师（就是钟将军）、武安王（就是关公）。木爻：茶延、枷锁、船神、草野、家堂、萧公（就是五圣）、东岳、树神、山神、九良星煞，和悬梁的鬼（就是上吊死的鬼）。水爻：观音、三官、北斗、龙王、祠山大帝、金龙四大王、水仙、杜氏夫人、河太、曹三、施相公、宋相公、晏公、坑厕的主、井泉童子、徐大将军、河伯水官，和溺死的魂。火爻：赵玄坛、五福大神、五显灵官、火德星君、三煞、天灯香愿、南堂五圣、灶神，和被火烧死的鬼。土爻：城隍、社庙里的神、五方贤圣、土皇、土地、太岁、螣蛇、皮肠大王，和瘟疫鬼。这里只是论五行各属哪些神，还要再把六兽放进官鬼里去寻，轻重也照这样比较，千万不要随口乱判是哪一位鬼神。
  The Pressure line on a line of a given phase gives that kind of god or ghost. A Metal line: a killing star, and further the Western Injury (the Five Roads); the Chief Official, the mourning spirits, the Seven Killings; Lord Zhang (Lord Liuwu) with the Golden Spirit and the Seven Killings, the Chief Official Jin Yuanqi, the vajra guardians in temples, ghosts who cut their own throats, and the vow of the chain; the Leaf God (Jiuzhuo), Master Zhong (General Zhong) and the King of Martial Peace (Guan Gong). A Wood line: the Tea Feast, the cangue and fetters, the boat god, the wilds, the household shrine, Lord Xiao (the Five Sages), the Eastern Peak, the tree god, the Mountain God and the Nine Good Stars, and the ghost of hanging from a beam (that is, one who hanged himself). A Water line: Guanyin, the Three Officials, the Northern Dipper, the Dragon King, the Great Emperor of Ci Mountain, the Golden Dragon Fourth Great King, the Water Immortal, Lady Du, He Tai, Cao San, Minister Shi, Minister Song, Lord Yan, the master of pit and privy, the youth of the well and spring, General Xu, the River Count and the water officials, and the souls of the drowned. A Fire line: Zhao Xuantan, the Great God of the Five Blessings, the Five Manifest Spirit Officials, the Fire Virtue Star Lord, the Three Killings, the heaven lamp and incense vow, the Five Sages of the Southern Hall, the Kitchen God, and ghosts burned to death. An Earth line: the City God, the gods of shrines and temples, the sages of the five quarters, the Earth Emperor, the Earth God, Tai Sui, the Coiling Snake, the Great King Pichang, and ghosts of epidemic. This only discusses which gods belong to which phase; one must further bring the six beasts into the Pressure line to search, weigh the light and the heavy in the same way, and on no account judge which ghost or god it is by idle talk.
- **LY.ILL.EL.47** 鬼值青龙：是东岳、家堂、茶筵、花煞、五圣、龙王、三官大帝、五路尊神，以及枷锁愿心、产亡的鬼。鬼值朱雀：是城隍、草野总管、天曹、华光（就是五显灵官）、广灵侯（就是南堂陆太君）、司令（就是灶神），以及旧欠愿心、牢里的鬼。鬼值勾陈：是土皇和五方贤圣，以及跌死的鬼。鬼值螣蛇：是妖怪，并且作犯，又是土、当方的土地、里社之神，以及产亡、缢死两种鬼。鬼值白虎：是伤司、五道、丧煞，赵玄坛和金神就是七煞，叶神就是九卓，以及刀伤、虎伤的鬼。鬼值玄武：是圣帝、曹三、河太、伏尸、坑厕、土井泉童子、杜氏夫人，采山就是草野三郎，以及溺死、偷盗两种鬼。要定下六神各是哪一位圣，还必须靠八卦和五行来排，哪一爻临在鬼上要看清楚，不要连累人白白花钱。
  The Pressure line on the Azure Dragon: the Eastern Peak, the household shrine, the Tea Feast, the Flower Killing, the Five Sages, the Dragon King, the Three Officials the Great Emperors and the honoured god of the Five Roads, together with ghosts of the cangue-and-fetters vow and of death in childbirth. On the Vermilion Bird: the City God, the Chief Official of the wilds, the Heavenly Bureau, Huaguang (the Five Manifest Spirit Officials), the Marquis Guangling (Lady Lu of the Southern Hall), the Director (the Kitchen God), together with ghosts of old owed vows and of the prison. On Hook Earth: the Earth Emperor and the sages of the five quarters, and the ghost of one who died by a fall. On the Coiling Snake: monsters, and it makes an offence; also the earth, the Earth God of the local quarter, the god of the village altar, and the two ghosts of death in childbirth and of hanging. On the White Tiger: the Injuring Office, the Five Roads, the Mourning Killing; Zhao Xuantan and the Golden Spirit are the Seven Killings; the Leaf God is Jiuzhuo; together with ghosts of knife wounds and tiger wounds. On the Dark Tortoise: the Holy Emperor, Cao San, He Tai, the buried corpse, the pit and privy, the earth, the youth of the well and spring, Lady Du; the Mountain Gatherer is the Third Lord of the wilds; together with the two ghosts of drowning and of theft. To fix which sage each of the six spirits is, one must still arrange them by the eight trigrams and the five phases, look closely to see which line sits on the ghost, and not burden people with empty spending of money.
- **LY.ILL.EL.48** 看神司，先推官鬼：官鬼属阳，是男人受伤；属阴，是女鬼。再看它临着哪一颗星、哪一个煞，就知道是什么在作祟。青龙上的鬼带天喜，可以说是花煞之神（天喜的排法：正月从戌上起，顺着数十二位）。鬼临白虎又带丧门，就说是丧煞为殃（丧门：正月戌、二月未、三月辰、四月丑、五月又到戌，只在这四个位上循环）。天火、天烛和朱雀同在，是五显灵官和灶皇（天火：正月子、二月卯、三月午、四月酉，循环；天烛：正月起在巳宫，顺着数到辰）；这个煞就是大朱雀，官鬼临上它，就是冒犯了五显灵官、灶神在作祟，而且天烛遇到发动时，做福、祈禳也大凶。玄武上的官爻临着天贼星或天盗煞，就是草野三郎（天贼：正月龙〔辰〕、二月鸡〔酉〕、三月虎〔寅〕、四月羊〔未〕、五月鼠〔子〕、六月蛇〔巳〕、七月犬〔戌〕、八月兔〔卯〕、九月猴〔申〕、十月牛〔丑〕、子月马〔午〕、丑月猪〔亥〕；天盗：正月亥、二月寅、三月巳、四月申，只在这四位上循环）。
  To look at a god or office, first work out the Pressure line: if it is yang, a man is harmed; if it is yin, it is a woman's ghost. Then look at which star and which killing it sits on, and one knows which haunting is the calamity. A ghost on the Azure Dragon with Heavenly Joy may be called the god of the Flower Killing (Heavenly Joy is set thus: the first month starts from Xu and runs forward through twelve places). A ghost on the White Tiger with Mourning Gate is said to be the Mourning Killing as calamity (Mourning Gate: the first month Xu, the second Wei, the third Chen, the fourth Chou, the fifth again reaching Xu, cycling only through these four positions). Heavenly Fire and Heavenly Candle together with the Vermilion Bird are the Five Manifest Spirit Officials and the Kitchen Sovereign (Heavenly Fire: the first month Zi, the second Mao, the third Wu, the fourth You, cycling; Heavenly Candle: the first month starts at the snake palace and runs forward, counting to the dragon); this killing is the Great Vermilion Bird, and a Pressure line on it is an offence against the Five Manifest Spirit Officials or the Kitchen God as haunting, and when it meets movement, making blessings or praying for exorcism is also very ominous. A Pressure line on the Dark Tortoise that sits on the Heavenly Thief star or the Heavenly Robber killing is the Third Lord of the wilds (Heavenly Thief by the twelve animals: the first month the dragon [Chen], the second the rooster [You], the third the tiger [Yin], the fourth the sheep [Wei], the fifth the rat [Zi], the sixth the snake [Si], the seventh the dog [Xu], the eighth the hare [Mao], the ninth the monkey [Shen], the tenth the ox [Chou], the Zi month the horse [Wu], the Chou month the pig [Hai]; Heavenly Robber: the first month Hai, the second Yin, the third Si, the fourth Shen, cycling only through these four).
- **LY.ILL.EL.49** 勾陈上的官鬼或螣蛇上的官鬼，又临着丧门或遇上吊客，都主伏尸土（吊客的排法：正月辰、二月丑、三月戌、四月未，只在这四位上轮；丧门的排法见上一条）。玄武上的鬼爻如果带着咸池、沐浴，就是杜氏夫人（沐浴：正月卯、二月子、三月酉、四月午，只在这四位上轮；咸池煞的排法相同，也是正月卯、二月子、三月酉、四月午）。鬼带勾陈，又临折煞，并且卦里的驿马发动，必定犯了途中跌死、伤亡（折煞：正月酉、二月午、三月卯、四月子，周而复始；驿马：正月申、二月巳、三月寅、四月亥，也是这四位轮）。玄武上的官爻又带天河煞，必是井中溺死的鬼，不然就必是江河里淹死的人（天河煞：正月从辰上起，顺行十二位）。
  A Hook Earth Pressure line or a Coiling Snake Pressure line that also sits on Mourning Gate or meets Guest of Condolence in every case governs the earth of a buried corpse (Guest of Condolence is set thus: the first month Chen, the second Chou, the third Xu, the fourth Wei, turning only through these four positions; Mourning Gate is set as in the previous entry). If a Dark Tortoise Pressure line carries Xianchi and Bath, it is Lady Du (Bath: the first month Mao, the second Zi, the third You, the fourth Wu, turning only through these four positions; the Xianchi killing is set in the same way, also the first month Mao, the second Zi, the third You, the fourth Wu). If the ghost carries Hook Earth and also sits on Breaking Killing, and the Post Horse in the hexagram moves, it surely offends a death by a fall or an injury on the road (Breaking Killing: the first month You, the second Wu, the third Mao, the fourth Zi, cycling round and round; Post Horse: the first month Shen, the second Si, the third Yin, the fourth Hai, also turning through these four positions). If a Dark Tortoise Pressure line also carries the Heavenly River killing, it is surely the ghost of one drowned in a well, or else of one drowned in a river (Heavenly River killing: the first month starts from Chen and runs forward through twelve places).
- **LY.ILL.EL.50** 朱雀上的官鬼又值着官符，就说是总管在作祟（官符：正月从午上起，顺行十二位）。鬼带刀砧，或遇上羊刃，就是伤司（刀砧煞：正月从午上起，顺行十二位；羊刃煞按日干：甲日在卯、乙日在辰、丙戊日在午、丁己日在未、庚日在酉、辛日在戌、壬日在子、癸日在丑）。太岁就是当年；鬼值太岁若带着黄旌，就是后土，后土就是土皇（黄旌星：正月戌、二月未、三月辰、四月丑，只在这四位上轮）。官鬼临着贵人或者天喜，就是萧堂五圣（天乙贵人：甲、戊、庚日在牛羊〔丑未〕，乙、己日在鼠猴〔子申〕，丙、丁日在猪鸡〔亥酉〕，壬、癸日在蛇兔〔巳卯〕，辛日遇马虎〔午寅〕；天喜的排法见前）。
  A Pressure line on the Vermilion Bird that also meets the Official Talisman is said to be the Chief Official haunting (Official Talisman: the first month starts from Wu and runs forward through twelve places). A ghost carrying the Chopping Block, or meeting the Goat Blade, is the Injuring Office (Chopping Block killing: the first month starts from Wu and runs forward through twelve places; Goat Blade killing by day stem: a Jia day at Mao, a Yi day at Chen, a Bing or Wu day at Wu, a Ding or Ji day at Wei, a Geng day at You, a Xin day at Xu, a Ren day at Zi, a Gui day at Chou). Tai Sui is the year itself; a ghost on Tai Sui, if it carries the Yellow Banner, is then the Sovereign Earth, and the Sovereign Earth is the Earth Emperor (Yellow Banner star: the first month Xu, the second Wei, the third Chen, the fourth Chou, turning only through these four). A Pressure line on the Noble or on Heavenly Joy is the Five Sages of the Xiao Hall (Tianyi Noble: Jia, Wu and Geng days at the ox and sheep [Chou, Wei], Yi and Ji days at the rat and monkey [Zi, Shen], Bing and Ding days at the pig and rooster [Hai, You], Ren and Gui days at the snake and hare [Si, Mao], and Xin days meeting the horse and tiger [Wu, Yin]; Heavenly Joy is set as before).
- **LY.ILL.EL.51** 鬼值三丘，或者临着五墓，就是坟墓（三丘：春丑、夏辰、秋未、三冬逢戌；五墓从三丘的对宫取，病人做福也难留）。土鬼带着暗金煞，或者值着羊刃星，是难产而亡、带血的鬼（暗金煞：寅、申、巳、亥的落在巳，子、午、卯、酉的落在酉，辰、戌、丑、未的落在丑；暗金对产妇最难当）。冲到病人的生年（年庚）就叫撞命：撞有天对和地冲的分别，天对是甲庚、乙辛、丙壬、丁癸，地冲是子午、丑未、寅申、卯酉、辰戌、巳亥；天对又地冲是真撞命（比如病人丙子年生，卦里壬午鬼动），只有地冲而天干大不对的（比如庚午鬼）是傍撞命，只有天对而地不冲的不算撞命；鬼值年建，就说是太岁。
  A ghost on the Three Mounds, or on the Five Tombs, is then a grave (Three Mounds: spring Chou, summer Chen, autumn Wei, the three winter months meeting Xu; the Five Tombs are taken from the opposite palace of the Three Mounds, so that even when the sick person performs blessing rites he can hardly be kept). An Earth ghost carrying the Hidden Metal killing, or sitting on the Goat Blade star, is the bloody ghost of one who died in childbirth (Hidden Metal killing: for Yin, Shen, Si and Hai it falls at Si; for Zi, Wu, Mao and You at You; for Chen, Xu, Chou and Wei at Chou; Hidden Metal is the hardest for a woman in childbirth to bear). A clash on the year of birth (nian geng) of the sick person is called a collision with life: collision has the distinction of heaven-opposition and earth-clash; heaven-opposition is Jia-Geng, Yi-Xin, Bing-Ren and Ding-Gui, and earth-clash is Zi-Wu, Chou-Wei, Yin-Shen, Mao-You, Chen-Xu and Si-Hai; heaven-opposition together with earth-clash is a true collision with life (for example, the sick person was born in the Bingzi year and a Renwu ghost moves in the hexagram); earth-clash with the stems greatly unmatched (for example a Gengwu ghost) is a side collision with life; heaven-opposition without earth-clash is not a collision with life; if the ghost sits on the year's branch, one says it is on Tai Sui.
- **LY.ILL.EL.52** 官鬼临着华盖，就是僧道的魂（华盖：正月戌、二月未、三月辰、四月丑，只在这四位上周而复始；咸池煞已写在前面）。鬼值咸池，就是邪淫的鬼。鬼值天刑煞，必定是犯了被责打死的鬼（天刑：正月从辰上起，逆行十二位）；如果临着天狱煞，就是牢里的鬼（天狱煞：正月遇亥，二月申，三月龙蛇〔即巳〕，四月寅，五月循环又到亥，周而复始定它是哪一路神）；鬼临劫煞，或者刀砧煞，必定是自刎而亡的（劫煞的起法和天狱煞相同，刀砧煞见前）。
  A Pressure line sitting on Huagai is the soul of a monk or Daoist (Huagai: the first month Xu, the second Wei, the third Chen, the fourth Chou, cycling round and round through only these four positions; the Xianchi killing has been set out earlier). A ghost on Xianchi is a ghost of lewdness. A ghost on the Heavenly Punishment killing is surely one who offended by dying of flogging (Heavenly Punishment: the first month starts from Chen and runs backward through twelve places); if it sits on the Heavenly Prison killing, it is a ghost in prison (Heavenly Prison killing: the first month meets Hai, the second Shen, the third the dragon or snake [that is, Si], the fourth Yin, the fifth month circles round and reaches Hai again, cycling round and round it fixes which spirit it is); a ghost on the Robbery Killing or the Chopping Block killing is surely one who died by cutting his own throat (the Robbery Killing is set in the same way as the Heavenly Prison killing, the Chopping Block killing as before).
- **LY.ILL.EL.53** 这套用星煞认鬼神的办法，是玄机里真正妙的诀窍，千金也不换，不要乱传。古时候正的（正统的）大概全天下都一样，现在的邪祟却各按本地不同；这一章是苕城新写的，别的郡的神司不同，凡是吴下的人，可以照这个来断，如果在别的地方问卜，还应该另外详细查。
  This way of recognising ghosts and spirits by the stars and killings is the truly marvellous secret of the mysterious mechanism, not to be traded for a thousand pieces of gold, and not to be spread about. In antiquity the orthodox ones were, on the whole, the same all under heaven, but now evil hauntings each follow their own locality; this chapter was newly written at Tiaocheng, and the gods and offices of other prefectures differ, so that for all people of Wu one may judge by this, and if divining in another place, one should look into it separately in detail.
- **LY.ILL.EL.54** 病人占问是哪路鬼神的时候，术士随口乱判是哪位神司，病家听信了就宰杀牲畜，把有限的家财倾尽，病未必能好，还给来生造下恶业，连占卜的人也难免。所以解禳按病症来，请僧道念经、斋戒，不用宰杀：瘟疫是上帝降下的，全家一起吃素，虔诚地请戒行严的僧人持经，自然消解，或者请童男礼拜也能得吉；疟疾或有鬼邪，依通书的样式设灶神就能送走；胎产是九天圣母管的，请道士诵玉枢经；怪病、倚草附木的妖，请僧人念观音经；冤业相寻，用梁皇忏（原文作「梁皇职」）解；亡魂出现，用地藏经超度；火殃降下，选火闭日或火收日，请道士诵火德经；精魅所临，大的告天师请法，小的诵真武经驱走；得膈噎症的人，布施食物、吃淡口，能超度饿鬼；屠宰索命的，只有戒杀放生才能免；妄罚誓愿、冒犯天曹的，要设斋祭奠，勾消旧账，穷得还不起愿的可以向天曹递疏、叩城隍退回，富家许过猪羊牛的愿，如今穷了可以做粉牲、纸钱纸马，到天库地库勾消也行；动土兴工出了事要告土，按那个方位设坛，没力量的用粉团斋殷太岁；经商、江湖上的诵三官经，也保读书人的功名；持玉皇经、虔诚祝告药师佛求今世的延年；金刚经作来生的福利；求嗣立梓童清坛，婴幼要念大洞尊经；毁平坟墓、社坛的，不是求神能除，只有改修才能解；侮慢神明的要皈依三宝或许能散；侵占祠宇的只有修复，旧庙才得安全。易卦分类的是万物之情，不能全部备写，卜筮通神明之德，可以自己参详。
  When a sick person divines about which ghost or spirit it is, the diviners wrongly judge the gods and offices at random, the sick household believes them and slaughters animals, exhausting its limited household means; the illness is not necessarily cured, and evil karma is made for the next life, which even the diviner can hardly escape. So exorcism follows the illness, inviting monks and Daoists to recite scriptures and keep fasts, without slaughter: epidemic is sent down by the Supreme Emperor, and the whole household eats vegetarian together and reverently invites a monk who keeps the precepts to recite the scripture, and it dissolves of itself, or boys performing worship can also bring good fortune; malaria, or where there are ghosts and evil, can be sent away by setting up the Kitchen God in the form of the almanac; childbirth is presided over by the Holy Mother of the Nine Heavens, and a Daoist is invited to recite the Yushu Scripture; for strange illnesses and demons that lean on grass and cling to trees, a monk chants the Guanyin Scripture; where grievances and karma pursue each other, the Liang Emperor rite (the text has 'Liang Emperor office') resolves it; where a dead soul appears, the Earth Store Scripture delivers it; if a fire calamity descends, choose a Fire Close day or a Fire Receive day and invite a Daoist to recite the Fire Virtue Scripture; where sprites have come, if great, appeal to the Heavenly Master to request rites, and if small, recite the Zhenwu Scripture to drive them away; one with a choking obstruction of the gullet gives food and keeps a bland mouth, which can deliver the hungry ghosts; where slaughtered animals demand a life, only abstaining from killing and releasing life can avoid it; those who rashly swore oaths and vows and offended the Heavenly Bureau must set a fast and offering to cancel the old debt, and one too poor to redeem the vow can present a memorial to the Heavenly Bureau and kowtow to the City God to return it, and a rich family that vowed pigs, sheep and oxen and is now poor can make flour animals, paper money and paper horses, and cancelling at the Heavenly Treasury and Earthly Treasury will do; when digging and building bring trouble, report to the Earth and set up an altar according to that quarter, and those without means offer flour balls as a fast to Tai Sui Yin; merchants and those on rivers and lakes recite the Three Officials Scripture, which also protects scholars' careers; reciting the Jade Emperor Scripture and reverently praying to the Medicine Buddha seeks long life in this world; the Diamond Sutra makes blessings for the next life; to seek an heir set up a pure altar to Zitong, and for infants recite the Great Cavern Honoured Scripture; where tombs and earth altars have been levelled, praying to the gods cannot remove it, and only rebuilding can resolve it; those who have insulted the gods must take refuge in the Three Jewels and may perhaps be dispersed; for encroaching on a shrine, only restoration, and then the old temple is safe. The hexagrams of the Yi classify the natures of all things and cannot all be written out in full; divination communicates with the virtue of the spirits, and one may examine it for oneself.
- **LY.ILL.EL.55** 请僧接道是为了招请贤者，要看的是应爻上站着什么。以应爻为主，以福德（子孙）为凭。应爻上是子孙（福德），这个僧道确实是受了戒、有行持的善人；父母临着应，法术精深专一；官鬼临着应，神鬼都敬服；兄弟临着应，多奸诈虚伪、常常阻误；妻财临着应，是贪婪图利、好色营财的人，不是能通天彻地的人。子孙临白虎，是恶人，心里容不下凶险；福德带青龙，是善人，有慈善，心里常存德行。占卦只看应爻，就知道休咎。
  To invite monks and Daoists in order to summon the worthy, look at what stands on the Other line. The Other line is the main line, and Output (the fortune spirit) is the ground. Output on the Other line: he is a good man who has received the precepts and has real conduct; Resource on it: his rites and arts are refined and exclusive; Pressure on it: gods and ghosts admire and submit; Peer on it: crafty, deceitful and false, and often blocking and erring; Wealth on it: a man greedy for profit, lustful and scheming for wealth, not one of those who penetrate heaven and earth. Output on the White Tiger is a wicked man; Output (the fortune spirit) with the Azure Dragon is a good man, compassionate, whose heart always keeps virtue. In divining, look only at the Other line, and one knows good and ill.
- **LY.ILL.EL.56** 法事成不成，要看几个关口：官鬼绝，神天不接受；卦是六冲，僧道没有缘分，所以六冲的卦不用。子孙用神管着自家的事，诵经宣偈要念得琳琅悦耳，如果遇到冲破，就只是白诵、成了谎话；僧道所在的应位是占卜人的用神，金经实偈要诚敬地宣扬，如果被冲，就白念、成了虚文；应爻落空，穿着袈裟礼拜也没有用，没有子孙，忏悔礼拜也是徒然。官爻主设醮的场地，没有鬼就没有因果，官空就没有德也没有功；子孙乘旺，斋坛的功德没有亏欠；福德掌管醮事，主事功有力。设斋修善的时候，福动却变成了鬼，就是想亲近反而疏远了；作福祈恩的时候，官鬼来克世爻，就是求荣反而得辱——都是要人感应、用心与天神相合，怎么可以亵渎而不加谨慎呢。
  Whether the rite succeeds depends on several points: if the Pressure line is cut off, the gods and heaven do not accept; if the hexagram is a Six-Clash, monks and Daoists have no affinity, so a Six-Clash hexagram is not used. Output as the Subject governs the affairs of my household, and reciting scriptures and proclaiming verses must be delivered in ringing tones; if it meets clash or break, the reciting is in vain and becomes lying words; the Other position of the monk or Daoist is the Subject of the one divining, and the golden scriptures and true verses must be proclaimed with sincerity and reverence; if it is clashed, the reciting is wasted and becomes an empty text; if the Other line is Void, what use is the robe and the bowing, and if there is no Output, penance and prostration are in vain. The Pressure line governs the site of the ritual; with no ghost there is no cause and effect, and with the Pressure line Void there is no virtue and no merit; with Output riding strength, the merit of the fast altar has no deficiency; Output holds the power of the ritual, and the work has strength. In holding a fast and cultivating good, if Output moves and changes into a ghost, then seeking closeness one grows distant; in performing blessing rites and praying for grace, if the Pressure line comes to control the Self, one seeks honour and gets shame instead; all of it asks for response and for the heart to accord with the heavenly gods, so how may one profane it without taking care?
- **LY.ILL.EL.57** 酬愿还赛、解厄，以福神（子孙）为主，以用象为凭。因为遭了灾要酬愿，就找福德；吉凶还要辨用爻得不得令。用爻弱而忌神兴，灾没有退；忌神衰而用爻旺，病患能消。父母发动克子孙，恩典浅薄承受不住；财爻发动生官鬼，邪魅就招来了。世爻旺、子孙强、官鬼受制，祭神之后就再没有妖祟；世是自己，旺则病必痊愈，子孙是福，强则压住鬼祟——这个强了，别的就必弱，福旺鬼将除，赛神之后保平安，解厄完了没有咎，禳解就吉，请祷就没有殃。
  In redeeming vows and lifting calamity, Output (the fortune spirit) is the main line and the Subject image is the ground. Because of a calamity one redeems a vow, so look to Output; for ill or good one must still distinguish whether the Subject line has gained or lost the season. With the Subject weak and the Adversary rising, the calamity has not retreated; with the Adversary weak and the Subject strong, the affliction can dissolve. If Resource moves and controls Output, the great grace is shallow and cannot be borne; if Wealth moves and generates Pressure, evil spirits are invited. With the Self strong, Output strong and the Pressure line held in check, after sacrificing to the gods there is no further evil; the Self is me, and if strong the illness will surely be cured; Output is fortune, and if strong it suppresses the ghostly haunting, and where this is strong the rest must be weak: the fortune strong, the ghost is about to be removed; after giving thanks to the gods peace is assured, when lifting the calamity is done there is no blame, and exorcism then is auspicious and petitioning and praying has no misfortune.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.50** 日辰带着鬼、来生合世爻，就该祈祷。看世爻带的是哪一亲，就知道该用什么办法治：带父母，要靠经、箓、符、醮来保扶；带子孙，要用药调理；带兄弟，要戒暴怒、节制饮食；带财爻，要好好休养；带官鬼，要祈祷鬼神。要知道该求哪一路神鬼，两家的办法不同，见分歧。
  When the Day carries Pressure and generates or combines with the Self line, one should pray. What the Self line carries shows the cure: Resource, scriptures, registers, talismans and rites; Output, medicine; Peer, curbing temper and moderating food; Wealth, good rest; Pressure, prayer to the ghosts and spirits. Which spirit or ghost to seek is found differently by the two commentaries (see the dispute).
- **LY.ILL.HJC.85** 占病，子孙、官鬼两爻都空，是吉兆；或者两爻都衰弱安静、没有冲也没有并，这病自己会好，不用吃药。如果两爻都动，不是药不对病，而是有神祟隔在中间（王洪绪：神祟在作祸），所以没有功效，必须又吃汤药、又祈祷鬼神才会痊愈，俗话叫「外修内补」。
  In divining illness, when the Output and Pressure lines are both Void it is a lucky sign; or if both are weak and still, with neither clash nor joining, the illness heals of itself and no medicine need be taken. If both lines move, it is not that the medicine does not fit the illness but that spirits or a haunting stand between (Wang Hongxu: are working mischief), and so it does no good; he must take decoctions and also pray to the ghosts and spirits to be cured, which the common saying calls 'cultivate without and nourish within'.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.93** 向鬼神求福，是现在人们崇尚的事，古时候也是这样。可是不拿易的爻来核对，祭祀也是白办。占的时候，先看卦里的官爻，鬼神是什么情形就知道了。官鬼既能带来实实在在的灾祸，也能带来福气和保佑，所以看它就能知道鬼神的情形。
  Seeking blessing from ghosts and spirits is what people of the present day esteem, and it has been so since ancient times. But if it is not checked against the lines of the Yi, even the sacrificial rites are performed in vain. In divining, look first at the Pressure line in the hexagram and you will know the condition of the ghost or spirit. The Pressure line can bring real disaster and can bring blessing and protection, so looking at it tells you the condition.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.94** 鬼爻旺相是神，休囚是鬼；阳爻是男，阴爻是女。鬼爻临长生是少年的鬼，临帝旺是壮年的鬼，临衰弱是老年的鬼，临胎、养是小孩的鬼。乾宫的鬼在西北方，巽宫的鬼在东南方。
  A Pressure line that is strong is a spirit; resting or trapped, it is a ghost. Yang is male and yin female. A Pressure line at Birth is a young person's ghost, at Peak a ghost of the prime of life, at decline an old person's ghost, and at Womb or Nurture a child's ghost. A ghost of the Qian palace lies to the northwest, and a ghost of the Xun palace to the southeast.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.95** 按鬼所在的卦宫推：在乾宫，是许过点天灯、拜北斗的愿；在兑宫，是口头许的愿或者伤神；在坎宫，是北方朝拜的圣像；在艮宫，是城隍或宅里的土神；在离宫，是南边的殿宇；在坤宫，是土地神或坟墓陵园；在震宫，是树神，或者被杖打死的男鬼；在巽宫，一定是吊死的，或者癫狂跌倒而死的女人。把八宫仔细推敲，各路鬼自然会显应出来。
  Work it out by the palace in which the ghost lies. In the Qian palace there was a vow of a heavenly lamp or to the Dipper; in the Dui palace, a spoken vow or an injuring spirit; in Kan, the sacred image worshipped in the north; in Gen, the City God or the earth spirit of the house; in Li, the southern hall; in Kun, the Earth God or graves and tombs; in Zhen, a tree spirit or a male ghost beaten to death with a staff; in Xun, surely one who hanged himself, or a woman who fell in a fit. Work out the eight palaces carefully and the various ghosts will make themselves apparent.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.96** 按鬼爻带的六神推：带勾陈，是土神在作祸；带朱雀，是有人咒骂诅咒；带白虎，是血神，有带血的女伤，在阳爻上是凶暴恶死的鬼；带玄武，是死得不明不白的鬼，变成兄弟或从兄弟变出来的，是盗贼；带青龙，是善愿；带螣蛇，是施相之神。
  Work it out by the six spirits the Pressure line carries. With the Hook Earth, an earth spirit is making trouble; with the Vermilion Bird, curses and imprecations; with the White Tiger, the blood spirit, a woman's injury with blood, and on a yang line a violent ghost who died an ugly death; with the Dark Tortoise, a ghost who died in an unclear way, and if it changes into Peer or has come out of Peer, a thief or bandit; with the Azure Dragon, a good vow; with the Coiling Snake, the spirit of Shixiang.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.97** 按鬼所属的五行推：金是被刀兵所伤的鬼，旺的是伤神，衰的是伤司手下的部众；火，一定是灶神或者香愿，落在二爻是灶君，衰弱的午火是被烧死的鬼；木是枷锁，东方的神鬼，变金或从金变出来的是戴枷锁的鬼，带螣蛇是吊死的鬼，木变木再加白虎是被夹死的鬼；水是河伯江神，旺相是江神河伯，衰弱是落水的鬼，在震、巽宫或木变水，是从船上掉下水淹死的鬼。
  Work it out by the element the ghost belongs to. Metal is a ghost wounded by blade and weapon: strong, an injuring spirit; weak, the followers of the Injuring Office. Fire is surely the Kitchen God or an incense vow: on the second line the Kitchen God, and a weak Wu Fire is a ghost burned to death. Wood is cangue and lock, a spirit or ghost of the east: changing into Metal or coming from Metal, a ghost in shackles; with the Coiling Snake, a hanged ghost; Wood changing into Wood with the White Tiger added, a ghost pressed to death. Water is the Lord of the River and the river god: strong, a river god; weak, a ghost who fell into the water; in the Zhen or Xun palace or Wood changing into Water, a ghost who fell from a boat and drowned.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.98** 鬼爻是土的要分清是哪一类：阴爻上是阴土，阳爻上是阳土；在震宫、巽宫，或者从木变出来的，是树头（木头）上的土；落在应爻、冲世爻的位置上，或者日辰带着土鬼来冲来克，是飞来的土；日、月、动爻、变爻都带土，是五方的土。
  Where the ghost is an Earth line, tell which kind: on a yin line, yin earth, on a yang line, yang earth; in the Zhen or Xun palace, or coming from a change from Wood, earth at the head of a tree (wood-head earth); standing on the Other line and clashing the Self line, or with the Day carrying an Earth ghost that clashes or controls, flying earth; if the Day, Month, moving line and changed line all carry Earth, earth of the five directions.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.99** 鬼的墓，是埋着的尸骨在作祸；财的库，是藏着的神不得安宁。看法：鬼爻属金，卦里有丑爻发动，就是鬼的墓；妻财属木，卦里有未爻发动，就是财的库；别的照这样推。但是不伤到世爻，就不会作祸。
  The ghost's tomb means a buried corpse is making trouble; Wealth's storehouse means a hidden spirit is unsettled. How to see it: if the Pressure line is Metal and a Chou line in the hexagram moves, that is the ghost's tomb; if the Wealth line is Wood and a Wei line in the hexagram moves, that is Wealth's storehouse; work out the others the same way. But if it does not harm the Self line, it does no harm.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.100** 因为修造、动土出了事，是土鬼碰上了勾陈：落在初爻是挖井动土，二爻是砌灶动土，三、四爻是门口动土，五爻是路边动土，六爻是篱笆墙下动土；在兑宫是挖池动土，艮宫是筑墙动土，坤宫是造坟动土，坎宫加玄武是挖厕坑动土。以上如果带朱雀，就是动土的时候暗中有口舌和咒诅，或者有人多嘴惹事，因此成了祸。
  Trouble from building or breaking ground is an Earth ghost meeting the Hook Earth: on the first line, digging a well; the second, building a stove; the third and fourth, breaking ground at the door; the fifth, by the road; the sixth, beneath the fence or wall; in the Dui palace, digging a pond; in the Gen palace, building a wall; in the Kun palace, building a grave; in the Kan palace with the Dark Tortoise, digging a privy pit. If any of these carries the Vermilion Bird, then at the time of breaking ground there were hidden quarrels and curses, or someone talked too much and stirred up trouble, and this became the misfortune.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.101** 按鬼落在哪一爻推：水鬼在初爻，冒犯了井神；火鬼在二爻，触犯了司命（灶神）；鬼在三爻（门），冒犯了家堂的香火；鬼在五爻（路上），要求五路神，金鬼是五道、火鬼是五圣、木鬼是五郎神众、水鬼是五路神司，衰弱就是路边的鬼；鬼在四爻，遇到冲世，一定是出门撞见的；鬼在六爻，逢月建生合，是远方的神来干犯，月建、日辰生合，是天神那里有愿。
  Work it out by the line on which the ghost falls. A Water ghost on the first line has offended the well god; a Fire ghost on the second has offended the Director of Fate (the Kitchen God); a ghost on the third (the gate) has offended the incense and fire of the household shrine; on the fifth (the road), seek the Gods of the Five Roads: a Metal ghost is the Five Paths, Fire the Five Sages, Wood the Five Lords and their company, Water the Officers of the Five Roads, and if weak a ghost of the roadside. A ghost on the fourth line meeting a clash with Self was surely met on going out; a ghost on the sixth line meeting a combination with the Month is a spirit from far away, and if the Month and the Day generate or combine with it there is a vow to a heavenly spirit.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.102** 拿卦身来推是什么人的鬼：鬼克卦身，是冤家债主的鬼，一定难以退去，病也不容易好；卦身克鬼，是妻妾一类女人的鬼；卦身生鬼，是晚辈、小孩、僧人道士的鬼（子孙）；鬼生卦身，是祖宗、长辈、父母的鬼（父母）；两者比和，是兄弟朋友的鬼，阴爻上是妯娌姊妹的鬼；没有生、合、克、刑，也一定是兄弟朋友。
  Work out whose ghost it is by the Body. A ghost that controls the Body is the ghost of an enemy or creditor, hard to drive off, and the illness will not easily settle; the Body controlling the ghost is the ghost of a wife, concubine or woman; the Body generating the ghost is the ghost of a junior, child, monk or Daoist (Output); a ghost generating the Body is the ghost of an ancestor, elder or parent (Resource); where the two are in harmony it is the ghost of a brother or friend (Peer), and on a yin line of a sister-in-law or sister; with no generating, combining, controlling or punishing, it is surely a brother or friend.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.103** 鬼带刑爻，一定是没有善终的鬼。怎么死的：旧注按鬼的地支分，辰是被墙壁压死，午是被热汤烈火烫死烧死，酉是被刀箭所伤，亥是投河落水，寅也许是被老虎咬死，巳是被蛇咬伤，戌是被狗咬，丑是被牛顶；旺相的成了神道，像刘郡王、猛将这样的神。王洪绪说要按鬼所属的五行去推是怎么死的。
  A ghost carrying a punishing line is surely one who did not die a good death. How he died: the old commentary goes by the ghost's branch — Chen, crushed by a wall; Wu, scalded or burned by hot water or fire; You, wounded by blade or arrow; Hai, threw himself into a river or fell into water; Yin, perhaps devoured by a tiger; Si, bitten by a snake; Xu, bitten by a dog; Chou, gored by an ox — and if strong it becomes a spirit-way, like the god Prince Liu, the Fierce General. Wang Hongxu says to work out the manner of death from the element the ghost belongs to.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.104** 鬼爻不上卦，就看它伏在哪一爻下面，便知道是什么鬼祟：伏在父母爻下，是家里已故的祖先；伏在子孙（福神）爻下，是小孩一类；伏在空亡的爻下，是没有人收管的鬼，是孤魂野鬼；如果临在绝爻上，是没有人祭祀的鬼。
  If the Pressure line is absent from the hexagram, see under which line it is hidden and you will know what kind of ghost it is: hidden under the Resource line, the family's forebears; under the Output (fortune spirit) line, children and the like; under a Void line, a ghost that no one keeps, a lonely wandering ghost; and if it meets an Extinction line, a ghost to whom no one sacrifices.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.105** 鬼落在日辰上，一定是刚死不久的亡灵：卦里没有官鬼、日辰是鬼的，一定是刚死的人在作祸；日辰是鬼、卦里又有鬼的，是因为祈祷，近来新许下的愿还没有还。
  A ghost that falls on the Day is surely a newly dead spirit: if there is no Pressure in the hexagram and the Day is the ghost, a newly dead person is surely making trouble; if the Day is the ghost and the hexagram also has a ghost, it is because of a prayer: a vow newly made lately has not been paid.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.106** 鬼自己入墓、带刑，一定是关在牢里的囚犯的鬼：比如在未日占卦，得到木爻的官鬼，叫做鬼自己入墓，一定是死在牢狱里的人的鬼；再带刑爻，一定是死于非命，或者死在坟墓之间；旺相又发动的，就是庙里的神。
  A ghost that enters its own tomb or is punished is surely the ghost of a prisoner in jail: if one divines on a Wei day and gets a Wood Pressure line, the ghost is said to enter its own tomb, and it is surely the ghost of one who died in prison; if it also carries a punishing line, he surely died an unnatural death or died among graves; and if strong and moving, it is a temple god.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.107** 旁边的财爻来合鬼，一定是月下私会的情人；应爻位上的兄弟来生它，是同社的好友。财爻发动来合鬼爻，或者财变鬼、鬼变财，自己互相合在一起的，一定是和病人私通的人在作祸；如果被世爻克，又带玄武，是因为通奸而死的鬼。
  A Wealth line beside it combining with the ghost is surely a lover met under the moon; a Peer on the Other line generating it is a good friend of the village society. If a Wealth line moves and combines with the Pressure line, or Wealth changes into Pressure or Pressure into Wealth so that they combine with each other, it is surely a person who had an illicit affair with the patient who is making trouble; if it is controlled by the Self line and carries the Dark Tortoise, it is the ghost of one who died because of adultery.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.108** 鬼爻发动，变出哪一个六亲，就按变出来的这个断：变成兄爻，是朋友、兄弟、妯娌、公婆这一类；变成鬼再加玄武，是盗贼；变成父母，是伯叔这些亲属，再加螣蛇，是他家屋里有工匠做了魇魅的手脚，使家里的人不得安宁；父母变成官，即使没有螣蛇，也是工匠作了弊。
  When the Pressure line moves and turns into one of the six relations, judge by what it turns into: into Peer, friends, brothers, sisters-in-law and parents-in-law; into Pressure with the Dark Tortoise, a thief; into Resource, uncles and other relatives, and with the Coiling Snake added, a craftsman in his house has worked a nightmare-spell so that the household is uneasy; if Resource changes into Pressure, even without the Coiling Snake, a craftsman has played a trick.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.109** 太岁上带鬼，是祖辈传下来的老规矩；日辰上并着官，是当初口头许下的心愿。如果太岁、日辰都带鬼，就是眼下许下要酬谢祖辈老规矩的愿，还没有办完。
  A ghost on the Tai Sui is an old custom handed down from the ancestors; Pressure joined to the Day is the original intention of a spoken vow. If the Tai Sui and the Day both carry the ghost, then a vow has been made for now to repay the ancestral custom, and it is not yet completed.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.110** 鬼爻持世，不是祖传的老规矩，而是先前口头许过愿、没有还；卦里没有鬼、世爻下面却伏着本宫的鬼，也是旧欠的愿，是嘴上答应了却没放在心上，所以招来责罚；鬼爻变出妻财，一定是斋戒修行没有做完，或者欠土煞的愿、破了戒；变成的财落空亡，是许了愿却没有戒口，有头没尾。
  When the Pressure line holds the Self position, it is not an old custom but a spoken vow made earlier and not yet paid; if there is no ghost in the hexagram but the ghost of the home palace is hidden beneath the Self line, it too is an old debt, words without the heart in it, and so blame has come; if the Pressure line changes into Wealth, a fast or observance was surely not completed, a debt owed to the earth spirits, or a precept was broken; and if the Wealth it changes into is Void, one made the vow but did not keep the abstinence of the mouth: a head with no tail.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.111** 鬼在住宅的位置上，住得不安稳；官落在应爻上，房子的朝向不顺。内卦的第二爻是宅：发动的鬼落在这里，住宅不安，常常有病；应爻带着鬼，这座宅子的朝向不利，应该改；旧注还说冲克二爻的爻也是这样。
  A ghost in the house position means the dwelling is unsettled; Pressure on the Other line means the orientation of the house is blocked. The second line of the inner trigram is the house: if a moving ghost sits there, the dwelling is unsettled and there is often illness; if the Other line carries the ghost, the orientation of the house is unfavourable and should be changed; the old commentary adds that a line that clashes with or controls the second line does likewise.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.112** 金在兑卦里发动，涉及佛像：兑是西方的卦，佛又是金身，所以遇到兑宫的金鬼，神佛那里一定有善愿。带青龙、勾陈，是要装銮塑像；带螣蛇，是想画像；变成父母，是描摹；不是这些，就是经文；变成财或福，是想修建房舍。
  Metal moving in the Dui trigram concerns a Buddha image: Dui is a trigram of the west and a Buddha has a body of gold, so meeting a Metal ghost in the Dui palace, there is surely a good vow to a god or Buddha. With the Azure Dragon and Hook Earth, it is to mount and mould an image; with the Coiling Snake, to paint a picture; changing into Resource, to copy or delineate; otherwise, scripture; changing into Wealth or Output, to build or repair a hall.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.113** 木在坎宫发动，冒犯了划船（龙船）的神：是船和桨的象，一定是划龙船的事，上三相公怪罪，把他们送走就吉利；在艮卦、巽卦里水鬼带青龙发动，以及木变水、水变木，鬼落在水上，也都是这个象。
  Wood moving in the Kan palace has offended the spirit of the rowed boat: it is the image of a boat and oars, surely a matter of rowing the dragon boat, and the Three Lords Above are displeased; seeing them off brings good fortune. In the Gen or Xun trigram, a Water ghost carrying the Azure Dragon and moving, or Wood changing into Water or Water into Wood, with the ghost on Water, is the same image.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.114** 水和土交错加在一起，在乾宫就是三元大帝（天官、地官、水官）：乾宫里土变水、水变土，又遇到官爻的，就是这个象；火和金互相发动，在兑卦就是五道伤官（五道是刀剑之神）：在兑宫里互相发动，又遇到官爻的，就是这个象。
  Water and Earth mingled together, in the Qian palace, are the Three Primes Great Emperor (the Officials of Heaven, Earth and Water): in the Qian palace, Earth changing into Water or Water into Earth and meeting a Pressure line is this image. Fire and Metal moving upon each other, in the Dui trigram, are the Five Paths and the Injuring Officer (the Five Paths are the spirit of blade and sword): moving upon each other in the Dui palace and meeting a Pressure line is this image.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.115** 第三爻落空，就是没有供香火的厅堂：占病时遇到第三爻空亡，他家不供奉香火；动了又落空、变出来也是空，像有又像没有；旺相而落空亡，供奉的器物不齐全。
  A Void third line means a hall where no incense or fire is offered: in divining illness, when the third line is Void, his family does not offer incense and fire; if it moves into Void or changes into Void, it is as if there were something and as if there were nothing; if strong yet Void, the sacrificial vessels are incomplete.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.116** 怪动有不吉利的灾祸：怪爻按起卦的月份取，一定要动了才成怪，不动就没有；带父母，一定有怪异的器物；加玄武，是偷来的东西；遇到这一爻发动，即使不是鬼爻，也一定是怪事；螣蛇又发动、落在鬼爻上，然后才可以说有怪异。旧注取位是季月落在初爻和六爻，仲月二爻和五爻，孟月三爻和四爻。
  When the strange line moves there is inauspicious misfortune: the strange line is taken by the month of casting, and it becomes strange only when it moves — if still, there is none. With Resource there is a strange object; with the Dark Tortoise added, stolen goods; whenever this line moves, even if it is not the Pressure line, it is a strange matter; and only when the Coiling Snake also moves and sits on the Pressure line can one say there is something strange. The old commentary takes the position as the first and sixth lines in the Ji months, the second and fifth in the Zhong months, and the third and fourth in the Meng months.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.117** 青龙遇到父母（文书）、一爻单独发动，可以断是经文：占有什么鬼神，不一定只看鬼爻，单独发动的那一爻也可以推。父母爻单独发动，是祖宗要求祭祀，带青龙就有善愿和经文；旧注还说，本宫的鬼爻伏在世爻下、又在胎、养、长生、帝旺的位置上，一定有刚死的人在讨要念经拜忏来超度，伏鬼属水的话棺材还暴露着，落空就不是。
  The Dragon meeting the document line, with a lone line moving, may be judged to mean scripture: in divining what ghost or spirit is involved, one need not rely only on the Pressure line, since a lone moving line can also be worked out. If Resource alone moves, the ancestors are asking for sacrifice, and with the Azure Dragon there is a good vow and scripture; the old commentary adds that if the Pressure line of the home palace is hidden beneath the Self line and stands at Womb, Nurture, Birth or Peak, a newly dead person is asking for scripture-chanting and rites of repentance to deliver him, and if the hidden ghost is Water the coffin is still exposed, and if it is Void it is not so.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.118** 螣蛇遇到官鬼、属阴的，梦里的事该推一推：鬼带螣蛇，一定有虚惊怪异；如果在阴宫、阴象，就有梦寐；冲克世爻、用爻的，一定是梦中见到的神祟；旧注还说螣蛇带着鬼发动，就是吊死鬼。
  The Snake meeting Pressure, of the yin kind: dreams should be worked out. A ghost on the Coiling Snake surely brings false alarms and strange things; in a yin palace and a yin image there are dreams; one that clashes with or controls the Self line or the Subject line is surely a spirit seen in a dream; and the old commentary adds that if the Coiling Snake moves with the ghost, it is a hanged ghost.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.119** 鬼动了又落入空亡，恐怕丢了孝敬追思的礼数：官爻发动落空、或变出来空，都主先人亡故的人里有丢了祭祀礼数的，或者遇到忌日没有摆祭；如果在别的宫、外卦，就是亲属里曾经有过祭祀的礼，现在却取消了，不设他的牌位；或者曾经送过纸钱纸帛，现在没有了。
  A ghost that moves into Void: it is feared the rites of filial remembrance have been neglected. If the Pressure line moves into Void or changes into Void, it means that among the departed forebears some rites of sacrifice have been lost, or no offering was set out on the anniversary of a death; if it is in another palace or the outer trigram, it is that among the kin there was once a rite of sacrifice which has now been dropped and his place is no longer set, or paper offerings used to be sent and now none is.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.120** 鬼安静地落在宅位（二爻）上又是木，家里停着裸露的棺材：木爻的官鬼安静地落在二爻，或者木鬼伏在父母爻下面，这家一定停着灵柩，把它抬走就安宁了；衰弱的话是座席，撤掉它，家宅安泰；旧注说照这样断，万无一失。
  A still ghost on the house position (the second line), being Wood, means the family has a coffin lying exposed: if a Wood Pressure line sits still on the second line, or a Wood ghost is hidden beneath the Resource line, the family surely has a coffin lying in the hall, and once it is carried away there will be peace; if weak, it is a seat or mat, and once it is taken away the household is at peace. The old commentary says that judged this way nothing can go wrong.
- **LY.ILL.YM.12** 常常试到：土鬼独自发动、又为疾病祈祷的，九死一生。有人说，用爻有气，祷告土神就能回生；用爻没有援助，就是开墓来葬死人，这话确实可信。注引《天元赋》说：土和煞一起发动，一定成为黄泉下的客；《补遗》说：夏天论，土爻带鬼发动，凶多吉少，祸难禳解。大体上东南土地薄，犯了土的忌讳、禳土而得生的，十个有六七个；西北土地厚，不怕土的禁忌，所以祈祷也没有功效。
  I have often tried it: where an Earth ghost moves alone and one prays over an illness, nine die and one lives. Some say that if the Subject line has force, praying to the earth spirit restores life, and if it has no help it is opening a tomb to bury the dead; this is truly credible. The note quotes the Tianyuan Fu: when Earth and killing stars both rise, he is sure to become a guest of the Yellow Springs; and the Buyi: in summer, an Earth line carrying a moving ghost is mostly ominous and seldom auspicious, and the misfortune is hard to exorcise. In general the earth of the southeast is thin, and of those who have offended earth taboos and exorcised the earth, six or seven in ten live; the earth of the northwest is thick and does not fear earth prohibitions, so prayer there has no effect.
- **LY.ILL.YM.42** 古时候的祭祀，不是那位神的主祭人就不当「尸」，不是鬼的同类就不享用祭品，所以没有越位的祭祀、没有轻慢的祭祀。后来的人有祟就做禳解、求福就去祈祷，鬼神的名目就多起来了；虽然这样，吉事有神帮忙，凶事有鬼凭依，史书传记里记的，并不全是虚妄。想知道鬼神的情形，也可以用占筮得到。天神地祇，就用官鬼来归类它的象；取象、画出它的样子，离不开两仪、四象、五行、八卦，再加上六神、诸星来给它成形，用有无来衡量，用上下来定位。探求这位鬼神的性情、所掌管的事，以及它怎么成为鬼神的，就可以推断了。注解释这三样：「性」是生前偏爱的性情，「司」是上帝命它掌管什么事，「成」是因为什么缘由成为鬼神。
  In the sacrifices of old, one who was not the rightful master did not stand as the impersonator, and a ghost did not savour what was not of its kind, so there were no sacrifices beyond one's station and no profane offerings. Later ages exorcised when there was a haunting and prayed for blessing, and so ghosts and spirits multiplied; even so, good fortune has spirits to help and misfortune has ghosts to possess, and what the histories and traditions record is not all false. To learn the state of ghosts and spirits, this too can be obtained by divination. For heavenly spirits and earthly deities, the image is classified by the Pressure line; the image is taken and drawn, and it does not go beyond the two modes, the four images, the five phases and the eight trigrams, together with the six spirits and the various stars to give it form, presence or absence to measure it, and above or below to place it. Seek the nature of the ghost or spirit, what it governs, and how it came to be one, and it can be worked out. The note explains the three: 'nature' is the disposition it favoured in life, 'what it governs' is what the Supreme Lord appointed it to govern, and 'how it came to be' is by what cause it became a ghost or spirit.
- **LY.ILL.YM.43** 官鬼爻属阳的，就是阳神；属阴的，就是阴神。属阴的官鬼，像王母、大悲、水母、太山、天妃、陈周、圣妃、蚕母、子母、催生、太君、娘娘这一类，也应当按地方、按人、按事去考察，这里只是举个大概。因为有的神是同一位而名字不同，这叫因地，比如在北方叫子母娘娘，在南方叫太君娘娘；男女有别叫因神，为什么事叫因事，比如问蚕的事，得到阴的官鬼，就是蚕母。
  A Pressure line that is yang is a yang spirit, and one that is yin is a yin spirit. Where the Pressure line is yin, as with the Queen Mother, the Great Compassionate, the Water Mother, Taishan, the Heavenly Consort, Chen and Zhou, the Holy Consort, the Silkworm Mother, the Child Mother, the Birth Hastener, the Grand Lady and the Ladies, one should also examine by place, by person and by matter; this only gives an outline. For there are spirits who are the same but named differently, which is 'by place': in the north she is the Lady of Child and Mother, in the south the Lady Grand Dame. Difference of man and woman is 'by spirit', and what the matter is about is 'by matter': for instance, when asking about silkworms, a yin Pressure line is the Silkworm Mother.
- **LY.ILL.YM.44** 官鬼属金的，就是武神。像天将、关帝、伍公、岳王、玄坛、总官、七煞、金刚、伤司、丧煞这一类；有的地方称作元帅、将军、金甲神的，以及靠威武善斗成了神的，都可以类推。
  If the Pressure line is Metal, it is a martial spirit, as with the Heavenly Generals, Emperor Guan, Lord Wu, Prince Yue, the Dark Altar, the Chief Official, the Seven Killings, the Vajra, the Injuring Office and the Mourning Killing; and those locally called marshals, generals and golden-armoured spirits, and those who became spirits by martial might and fighting courage, can all be reasoned by analogy.
- **LY.ILL.YM.45** 官鬼属水的，就是河、海、江的神。像普陀、真武、云台、北斗、海神、龙王、河神、大王、杨将、施相、天妃、晏公、祠山、周王、汪公、水母、水圣、井神、池神、水伤神这一类，以及因水而成神的，都可以类推。九河百川，各个地方各有掌管的神，百姓的尊称各不相同，要随着占卜人所在的民俗和风土，用五行八卦、六神诸星去取象，才算射中靶心。大体上水神、火神灵验显赫的多，没法一一细说，各地不一样：漕河尊大王，东海尊天妃，南河尊周王，洞庭尊湘妃，鄱阳是小姑，长江是杨四将军，各有各的分管。
  If the Pressure line is Water, it is the spirits of river, sea and great river, as with Putuo, Zhenwu, Yuntai, the Dipper, the Sea God, the Dragon King, the River God, the Great King, General Yang, Lord Shi, the Heavenly Consort, Lord Yan, Cishan, Prince Zhou, Lord Wang, the Water Mother, the Water Sage, the Well God, the Pond God and the water-wound spirits, and those who became spirits through water. For the nine rivers and the hundred streams each place has its own governing spirit, and the honorifics the common people give differ from place to place; following the customs and local ways of the one who divines, take the image by the five phases, the eight trigrams, the six spirits and the various stars, and one hits the mark. In general the spirits of water and fire are often powerfully manifest and cannot be set out in detail, differing from place to place: the grain-transport canal honours the Great King, the Eastern Sea the Heavenly Consort, the southern river Prince Zhou, Dongting the Xiang Consort, Poyang has the Little Aunt, and the Yangtze General Yang the Fourth, each with its own domain.
- **LY.ILL.YM.46** 官鬼属木的，就是文神，以及依托在草木上的精怪。像东岳、家堂、山神、树神、五圣、园神、九良、茶筵、三茅、九天、鲁班、花神、三郎、道君、文昌、南木神这一类，以及各地有依附山木、树木、器皿而成神的，都可以类推。
  If the Pressure line is Wood, it is a civil spirit, and the demons that lean on grass and cling to trees, as with the Eastern Peak, the household shrine, the Mountain God, the Tree God, the Five Sages, the Garden God, the Nine Good Ones, the Tea Feast, the Three Mao, the Nine Heavens, Lu Ban, the Flower God, the Third Lord, the Daoist Lord, Wenchang and the Southern Wood God, and those places where a spirit has arisen from a mountain, a tree or a vessel it clings to; all can be reasoned by analogy.
- **LY.ILL.YM.47** 官鬼属火的，就是火神，是掌管生育、雷电的。像离神、电神、南斗、火雷、韦陀、元坛、华光、五神、五赫、三煞、灶神、旗纛（？）、炉神、炮神、香火、南堂、利市、生育神这一类，以及因火而成神的，都可以类推。
